1b5(rgyud): brlabs pa'i dam tshig gi ye shes rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i khyad par dang ldan pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad/
1b6(rgyud): kyi rin po che'i cod pan khams gsum pa'i chos kyi rgyal por dbang bskur bar brnyes pa/de bzhin gshegs 
1b7(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi rdo rje dam tshig gi ye shes rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen po de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad 
1b8(rgyud): kyi ye shes kyi phyag rgya brnyes pa/de bzhin gshes pa thams cad kyi phyag rgya thams cad mnyam pa 
2a1(rgyud): @#//pa nyid thugs su chud cing las sna tshogs mdzad pa ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i sems can gyi khams thams cad kyi re ba thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa'i rdo rje'i las mdzad pa/thugs rje chen po 
2a2(rgyud): rnam par snang mdzad chen po rtag pa dus gsum du gnas pa'i sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bsten cing bsngags pa'i nor bu rin po che chen po dang ldan pa/kha 
2a3(rgyud): dog rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i dril bu btags pa/rlung gis bskyod pa'i dar gyi ba dan dang /me tog gi chun po dang /dra ba dang dra ba phyed pas klubs shing nye bar mdzes pa/chos kyi dbyings thams cad legs par yongs su dag pa/
2a4(rgyud): chos kyi dbyings kyi khang bzang 'od mtha' yas pa/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i gtsug lag khang me tog gi 'khri shing mchog gi snying po'i rgyan gyis bkod pa/'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud rgya mtsho rab tu bkram pa'i sprin 
2a5(rgyud): nam mkha'i khams yongs su khyab pa/de bzhin gshegs pas byin gyis brlabs pa/mtha' las 'das pa'i sangs rgyas 'khrung ba'i dbus/rnam par snang mdzad kyi snying po 'od gsal ba'i gzhal med khang /de bzhin 
2a6(rgyud): gshegs pa'i gnas/rdo rje snying po'i rgyan de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje/de bzhin gshegs pa mi 'khrung mi 'gog 'gog par mi 'gyur ba'i yan lag gi spyod yul yang dag pa'i mtha' gsol 
2a7(rgyud): ba/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi yul/chos kyi dbyings kyi snying po'i khang bzangs/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi ye shes kyi sku/de bzhin gshegs pa thamad cad kyi byang 
2a8(rgyud): chub kyi snying pos chen po'i pad ma la bzhugs so//de la sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi rdul phra rab rdul dang mnyam pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams dang /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 
2b1(rgyud): kyis yongs su bskor zhing mdun du bdar te chos ston par mdzad do//thog mar dge ba bar du dge ba/tha mar dge ba/don bzang po/tshig 'bru bzang po/ma 'dres pa yongs su rdzogs pa/yongs su dag pa/yongs 
2b2(rgyud): su dkar ba chos thams cad kyi tshul rnam par dag pa'i sgo ston par mdzad do//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje gnyis su med pa mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes sems 
2b3(rgyud): dpa'/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi ye shes kyi nyi ma/rnam par rgyal ba'i dam tshig 'byung gnas rdo rje snying po/chos thams cad ngo bo nyid kyis rnam par dag pa'i ye shes mnyam pa nyid brnyes pa/rgya mtsho 
2b4(rgyud): ltar zab pa/las sna tshogs pa'i phyag rgya'i ye shes thob pa/sku dang gsung dang thugs rje rnam mkha' lta bu/sku chen po rdo rje'i sku/mtha' las 'das pa'i sku/sku rin po che chos kyi sku/sprul pa'i sku/las mnyam 
2b5(rgyud): pa nyid kyi sku/byang chub kyi sems don yod pa/yongs su bsdus pa/dge ba 'phel ba/rgyal po chen po snang ba tog/bzhad pa po chos dang ldan pa shes rab 'khor lo/skad thams cad pa/sna tshogs pa can/
2b6(rgyud): bsrung ba po/gnod sbyin can mtshams sbyor ba/gtsug tor du gyur pa mchog ldan rdo rje can 'khor lo can ye shes can khyab bdag chen po/sku dang gsung dang thugs/rdo rje nam mkha'i dbyings thams cad du legs par 
2b7(rgyud): bzhugs pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad yang dag par 'du bar byed pa/rdo rje'i dbyings thams cad khong du chud pa'i ye shes sems dpa' nam mkha'i khams thams cad kyi rdul phra rab kyi rdul dang mnyam pa'i rdo rje'i 
2b8(rgyud): byin brlabs kyi ye shes snying po/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi bla na med pa'i rdo rje rin po che'i dbang bskur ba chen po'i dam tshig nam mkha'i khams thams chad du spros pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i mngon par 
3a1(rgyud): @#//shes pa mngon par byang chub par gyur pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag nyid kyi ngo bo rnam par dag pa de yis chos thams cad rang bzhin gyis dag pa nam mkha' thams cad la khyab pa po//gzugs 
3a2(rgyud): thams cad yongs su mthong ba'i ye shes kyis ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i sems can 'dul ba'i spyod pa mchog dang ldan pa/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams cad don yod pa'i bka' lung mdzad pas sems can thams 
3a3(rgyud): cad mnyam pa nyid bla na med pa'i las sna tshogs pa can/rnam snang mi 'gyur mi bskyod pa//rin chen tog te 'od byed pa//'od dpag med pa rgyal ba'i mgon//don yod pa ni las kun byed//de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
3a4(rgyud): cad kyi me long lta bu'i ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes la rab tu gnas pa/de bzhin gshegs pa mtha' yas cad kyi so sor rtog pa'i ye shes gnyis su med pa 
3a5(rgyud): brnyes pa/de bzhin gshegs pa/thams cad kyi bya ba grub pa'i ye shes yang dag par rjes su thob pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi dbyings rnam par dag pa/chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes 
3a6(rgyud): kyi spyod yul/de bzhin gshegs pa tham/cad kyi gnyis su med cing mnyam pa nyid dmigs pa'i ye shes yongs su dag pa mthar phyin pa/stong pa nyid dang de bzhin nyid dang /yang dag pa'i mtha' dang /mtshan ma med pa dang 
3a7(rgyud): smon pa med pa dang /tshangs pa'i gnas bzhi po dag gis sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyi yul du gyur pa/sangs rgyas kyi yul mnyam pa nyid la dmigs pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i thams cad rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug 
3a8(rgyud): chen po nyid la byin gyis brlabs nas/phyogs dang mtshams rnams su kun du gnas so//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi dbangs phyug gis/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la bka' stsal pa/'jam 
3b1(rgyud): dpal khyod kyis sangs rgyas thams cad dang rdo rje 'dzin pa rnams spyan drongs shig/bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /'jam dpal gzhon nu 'khor los sgyur ba chen po rab tu dgyes pa'i zhal ras kyis de 
3b2(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang /rdo rje 'dzin pa rnams spyan dang ba'i don du rdo rje se gol brdabs te de bzhin gshegs pa thams can bsdu ba'i snying po chen po 'di ched du brjod pa/bud d+ha sa mA dza AHhAHoM hU~M badzra sa mA dzaHde nas de 
3b3(rgyud): skad ces brjod pas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad dang /rdo rje 'dzin rnams kyis 'jig rten gyi khams me tog gi gzhi'i snying po'i rgyan bkod pa'i rgyud rab 'byam rgya mtsho'i sprin yang dag par mthong ngo //de nas yang 'jam dpal 
3b4(rgyud): gzhon nur gyur pas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad la/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams thams cad kyis lhan cig yang dag par sbyar nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi rdo rje blo'i ye shes yongs su bskyed de gnas 
3b5(rgyud): thams cad du rnam par gnas so//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam pa snang mdzad de bzhin gshegs pa thams//kye de bzhin gshegs pa thamasaHcad thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes 'khor los bsgyur ba chen po'i snying po bdag la yod pa de ngas bshad par 
3b6(rgyud): bya'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis byin gyis rlobs shig/b+h+ru~M/de nas/mi bskyod de bzhin gshegs pa dang //rin chen 'byung gnas de bzhin gshegs//de bzhin gshegs pa 'od dpag med//don yod grub pa de bzhin 
3b7(rgyud): gshegs//gzhan yang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams//lhan cig de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi thugs dpal gyi be'u la rab tu zhugs so//zhugs pa tsam gyis de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad 'khor lo la bde ba thams

4b1(rgyud): dpa'i gnas so//dri rnam par dag pa'i gnas gang yin pa de ni 'di lta ste byang chub sems dpa'i gnas so//ro rnam par dag pa'i gnas dang yin pa de ni 'di lta te byang chub sems dpa'i gnad so//reg rnam par dag pa'i gnas

4b6(rgyud): pa chen po nye bar bsags kyang na/yang dmyal ba la sogs pa'i ngan song rnams su ltung bar mi 'gyur ro//sdig pa rnams byas pa tsam na yang sdug bsngal med cing zad par 'gyur ro//gang gis 'di 'chang ba dang /nyi ma re re

5a1(rgyud): @#//snang mdzad kyis slar yang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi zhi ba'i chos nyid mngon par byang chub pa mngon par bsgrub pa 'di ston par mdzad do//rdo rje brtan pa nyid kyis rdo rje 
5a2(rgyud): mnyam pa nyid mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa'i byang chub chen po dang /don gcig pa nyid kyis don mnyam pa nyid mngon par byang chub ba'i byang chub chen po dang rang bzhin rnam par dag pas chos mnyam pa nyid mngon par
5a3(rgyud): byang chub pa'i byang chub che na po dang /rnam par rtog pa dang /rnam par mi rtog pa nyid kyis las thams cad mnyam pa nyid mngon par byung chub pa'i byang chub chen pi ste/'jam dpal gang la las chos kyi gnas bzhi po 'di
5a4(rgyud): nyan par byed pa dang 'dzin pa dang /'don pa dang /sgom par byed na ji srid byang chub snying po la gnas kyi bar du sdig pa thams cad la kun du spyod pa thams cad kyang sdig pa thams cad las 'das par 'gyur zhing myur du bla na
5a5(rgyud): med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par byang chub par 'gyur ro//AM/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das gdug pa thams cad 'dul ba de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pas chos thams cad mnyam pa nyidarnam par rgyal
5a6(rgyud): bas bsdus pa zhes bya ba shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mngon par sgrub pa bka' stsal pa/'dod chags kyi spros pa med pa'i phyir 'dod chags spros pa med pa'o//zhe sdang gi spros pa med pa'i phyir zhe sdang spros pa med pa'o//gti mug gi
5a7(rgyud): spros pa med pa'i phyir gti mug spros pa med pa'o//chos thams cad spros pa med pa'i phyir chos thams cad spros pa me dpa'o//shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa spros pa med pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol du phyi na pa spros pa med par rig
5a8(rgyud): par bya'o//'jam dpal gang la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa'i tshul 'di nyan pa dang 'dzin cing 'don la klog par byed na de 'dul ba'i dbang nye bar gzung nas khams gsum du skyes pa'i sems can rnams gsad par
5b1(rgyud): gyur kyang ngan song du 'gro bar mi 'gyur zhing myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs par byang chub par 'gyur ro//hUM/de nas bcom ldan 'das rang bzhin gyis rnam par dag pa'i chos nyid brnyes pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa slar yang 
5b2(rgyud): chos thams cad spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug ye shes kyi phyag rgya zhes bya ba'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul 'di bka' stsal pa/'dod chags thams cad rnam par dag pa'i phyir 'jig rten na yul thams cad rnam par 
5b3(rgyud): dag pa nyid du 'jug par 'gyur ro//zhe sdang thams cad rnam par dag ba'i phyir 'jig rten na sdig pa thams cad rnam par dag nyid du 'jug par 'gyur ba'o//chos thams cad rnam par dgapa'i phyir 'jig rten na chos thams cad 
5b4(rgyud): rnam par dag pa nyid du 'jug par gyur ro//thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes rnam par dag pa'i phyir jig rten na thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shas rnam par dag pa nyid du 'jog par 'gyur ro//shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 
5b5(rgyud): rnam par dag pa'i phyir 'jog rten na shes rab kyi pha rol tu ma phyin pa rnam par dag pa nyid du'ajug par 'gyur ro//'jam dpal gang la las shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa'i tshul 'di nyan par byed 'dzin cing 'don la sgom par 
5b6(rgyud): byed na de 'dod pa thams cad kyi nang na gnas kyang pad ma lta bur 'dod chags kyi skyon glo bur ba'i dri mas gos par mi 'gyur zhing myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par byang chub par 'gyur ro//hrIHde nas 
5b7(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das khams gsum pa thams cad kyi bdag po de bzhin gshegs pas slar yang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dbang bskur ba'i 'byung gnas ye shes snying po zhes bya ba shes rab kyi pha rol du phyinapa'i tshul bka' 
5b8(rgyud): stsal pa/dbang bskur byin pas khams gsum po thams cad kyi rgyal srid yongs su thob par 'gyur ro//don gyi sbyin pas thams cad kyi bsam pa yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro//chos kyi sbyin pas chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid 
6a1(rgyud): @#//thob par 'gyur ro//zang zing gi sbyin pas thams cad kyi lus dang ngag dang yid bde ba thob par 'gyur ro//trA+oM/de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ye shes kyi phyag rgya brnyes pa thams cad kyi khu 
6a2(rgyud): tshur 'chang pa de bzhin gshegs pa rtag pas slar yang de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi ye shes phyag rgya byin gyis brlabs pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul bka' stsal pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams
6a3(rgyud): cad kyi sku'i phyag rgya yongs su bzung bas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad nyid du 'gyur ba dang/gsung gi phyag rgya yongs su bzung pas chos thams cad thob pa'i don du 'gyur ba dang/thugs kyi phyag rgya yongs su bzung ma bas ting nge 'dzin thams
6a4(rgyud): cad thob pa'i don du 'gyurapa dang/rdo rje'i phyag rgya yongs su bzung a bas sku dang gsung dang thugs thams cad rdo rje mchog tu 'grub pa'i don du 'gyur te/'jam dpal gang la las shes rab kyi pha rol du pyin pa'i tshul 'di nyan par byed 
6a5(rgyud): 'dzin cing 'don la 'chang cing sgom par byed na de dngos grub thams cad dang/phun sum tshogs pa thams cad dang/ye shes thams cad dang/las thams cad sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje sems dpa' mchog gi dngos grub thams cad
6a6(rgyud): so sor thob par 'gyur zhing myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par byang chub par 'gyur ro//aHde nas bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad spros pa med pa'i de bzhin gshegs pas slar 
6a7(rgyud): yang rdo rje'i yi ge'i 'khor lo bskor ba zhes bya ba'i shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul bka' stsal pa/rang bzhin med pa'i tshul gyis chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang/chos thams cad mtshan ma med pa nye bar bzung ba'i phyir chos
6a8(rgyud): thams cad mtshan mas dben pa dang/smon pa las nye bar grol ba'i phyir chos thams cad smon pa med dang/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyi na pa yongs su dag pa'i phyir chos thams cad rang bzhin gyis 'ong gsal ba'o//oMHde nas
6b1(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor lo'i nang du gnas pa'i de bzhin gshegs pas slar yang 'khor lo chen por 'jug pa zhes bya ba'i shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul bka' stsal pa/rdo rje mnyam 
6b2(rgyud): pa nyid la zhugs nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi 'khor lor 'jug par 'gyur ba dang/don mnyam pa nyid la zhugs nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po'i 'khor lor 'jug par 'gyu pa dang chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid 
6b3(rgyud): la zhugs nas dam pa'i chos kyis 'khor lor 'jug par 'ag+h+yur pa dang/las mnyam pa nyid la zhugs na 'khor lo thams cad du 'jug par 'gyur ro//hU~M/de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa thams cad kyi 
6b4(rgyud): mchod pa'i cho ga rgyas pa'i snod de bzhin gshegs pas sla yang de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi mchod pa dam pa zhes bya ba'i shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul bka' stsal ba/byang chub kyi sems bskyed pas de bzhin 
6b5(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi mchod pa'i cho ga rgyas pa dang/sems can gyi khams thams cad yongs su skyob pas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'chod pa'i cho ga rgyas pa dang/chos thams cad yongs su bzung bas de bzhin 
6b6(rgyud): gshegs pa tha mas cad kyi mchod pa'i chog rgyas pa dang shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'bri ba dang/'brir 'jug pa dang/'dzin pa dang/'don pa dang brjod pa dang/sgom pa sngon du 'gro ba'i las kyi de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad 
6b7(rgyud): kyi mchod pa'i cho ga rgyas pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das gdug pa thams cad 'dul ba'i nus pa'i de bzhin gshegs pas slar yang sems can thams cad 'dul ba'i ye shes snying po zhes bya ba shes rab kyi pha rol du 
6b8(rgyud): phyin pa'i tshul 'di bka' stsal pa/sems can thams cad mnyam pa nyid pas khro pa mnyam pa nyid dang/sems can thams cad 'du ba nyid pas khro pa 'dul ba nyid dang /sems can thams cad chos nyid nyid pas khro ba chos 
7a1(rgyud): @#//nyid nyid dang //sems can thams cad rdo rje nyid pas khro ba rdo rje nyid de de rgyu ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar sems can thams cad 'dul ba'i byang chub ste/haHde nas bcom ldan 'das thams cad mnyam pa nyid la 
7a2(rgyud): rab tu gnas pa'i de bzhin gshegs pas slar yang chos thams cad mnyam pa nyidakyi mchog ces bya ba'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul 'di bka' stsal pa//thams cad mnyam pa nyid las shes rab kyi pha rol tu
7a3(rgyud): phyin pa mnyam pa nyid dang /thams cad kyi don nyid pas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa'i don nyid dang chos thams cad mnyam pas nyid pas shes rab kyi pharal tu phyin pa'i chos mnyam pa nyid dang /thabs cad las nyid pas
7a4(rgyud): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa'i las nyid du rig par bya'o//hrIHde nas bcom ldan 'dasde bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis slar yang de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad byin gyis rlob pa zhes bya ba'i shes rab kyi pha
7a5(rgyud): rol tu phyin pa'i tshul 'dibaka' stsal pa /byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po bdag nyid thams cad pas sems can thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po dang /rdo rje'i snying pos dbang bskur bas sems
7a6(rgyud): can thams cad rdo rje'i snying po dang /rin po che thams cad kyi dbang bskur bas sems can thams cad rin po che'i snying po dang /ngag thams cad la rab tu 'jug pas sems can thams cang chos kyi snying po dang /las thams
7a7(rgyud): cad byed pa'i sbyor bas sems can thams tsad las kyi snying po'o//hrIHde nas bcomaldan 'das mtha' las 'das shing mu med pa'i mthar phyin pa'i de bzhin gshegs pas slar yang mu med cing mthar phyin pa'i chos dang slar
7a8(rgyud): yang 'di'i rtog pa dang yongs su mthar thug pa byin gyis brlab pa'i don du chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid yongs su mthar thug pas byin gyis brlabs pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul 'di bka'
7b1(rgyud): stsal pa//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mtha' med pa'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mtha' med pa dang /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa mu med pa'i phyir de bzhin ga shegs pa thams cad mu med pa dang /shes rab gyi pha 
7b2(rgyud): rol tu phyin pa gcig pa nyid kyi phyir chos thams cad gcig pa nyid dang /shes rab kyi pha ral tu phyin pa yongs su mthar thug pa'i phyir chos thams cad yongs su mthar thug par 'gyur ba ste/'jam dpal gang la las shes 
7b3(rgyud): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul 'di nyan par byed 'dzin cing 'don la sgom par byed na de ni dag cing mthar phyin pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa mthar phyin pa la gnas pa nyid kyis sgrib pa thams cad las rnam par grol 
7b4(rgyud): bas de bzhin gshegs pa nyid dam rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid myur du thob par 'gyur ro//b+h+yoHde nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad de bzhin gshegs pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams ca kyi gsang ba'i chos nyid brnyes pa/
7b5(rgyud): chos thams cad spros pa med pas slar yang bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig ces bya ba rdo rje chos mnyam pa nyid shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'isgo dam pa thog ma dang tha ma dang /dbus las grol ba'i mchog 'di bka' 
7b6(rgyud): stsal pa//byang chub sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi 'dod chags chen po mchog tu grub pas bde ba chen po mchog tu grub par 'gyur ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' chenapo rnams kyi bde ba chen po mchog tu 'grub pas de 
7b7(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byang chub chen po mchog tu grub pa 'gyur ba dang /byang chu sems dpa' chen po rnamasakyi de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi byang chub mchog tu grub pas bdud sde chen po rab tu 'joms 
7b8(rgyud): pa mchog tu grub par 'gyur pa dang /byang chub sems dba' chen po rnams kyi bdud sde chen po rab tu mjoms pa mchog tu grub pas khams gsum po mtha' dag gi dbang phyug mchog tu 'grub par 'gyur ba dang /byang chub 
8a1(rgyud): @#//sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi 'jig rten gsum po mtha' dag gi dbang phyug mchog tu grub pas ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i sems can gyi khams yongs su skyob pa'i sems can thams cad la phan pa 
8a2(rgyud): dang bde ba'i mchog shin du bde ba chen po mchog tu grub par 'gyur ro//swaM/de rgyu ci'i phyir zhe na/ji srid 'khor ba'i gnas su ni//mkhas pa mchog rnams gnas gyur pa//de srid mnyam med sems can don//mya ngan mi 'da' byed 
8a3(rgyud): par nus//shes rab pha rol tu phyin pa'i thabs/ye shes byin gyis brlabs kyis 'grub//las ni thams cad rnam dag pas//srid pa dag par 'gyur phyir ro//chags sogs kyis 'dul 'jig rten ni//kun nas srid par 
8a4(rgyud): rtag sdig byed//de dag rnam par sbyang don du//srid pa'i bar du rang 'dul byed//ji ltar pad ma legs dmar pa//tshon gyi skyon gyis ma gos ltar//bag chags skyon gyi srid pa ru//rtag tu 'gro phan gos mi 'gyur//'dod chags chen 
8a5(rgyud): po rnam dag pa//bde ba chen po nor chen po//khams gsum dbang phyug thob nas su//de dag don ni brtan par byed/haH/'jam dpal gang la la shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul gyi sgo'i chos kyi rnam grangs 'di nyin re bzhin 
8a6(rgyud): du nang bar langs te/gang 'don pa dang nyan pa dang 'dzin pa dang /brjod par byed na nges bde ba dang /yid bde ba thams cad thob par 'gyur zhing bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig gi dngos grub mchog du gyur pa skye ba 'di nyid 
8a7(rgyud): la thob par 'gyur ro//hUM/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la bka' stsal pa/'jam dpal nyi shu rtsa lnga po 'di dag ni nges par shes rab kyi pha rol du phyi pa'i tshul gyi sgo ste 'di ltar/
8a8(rgyud): oM na maHsarba bung d+ha bo d+hi satwA nI/oM bo d+hi tsit+ta badzre/oM sa man ta b+ha dra tsa r+ye d+hi/oM tsan+di maN+DI oM a re ni ro d+hi oM dza ti bi brte/oM dzA ti pa ri war te/oM ma hA rA ga d+har+m+ma te/oM bIr+ya ka ba tse/oM sarba gA mi ni/oM badzra dri 
8b1(rgyud): D+ha ka ba tsi tai hUM phaT/oM sarba ta thA ga tai/oM swa b+hA ba shud d+he oM pradz+nyA pA ra mi ta dz+nyA na bi shud d+he/oM karma bi sho d+ha ne hUM/oM ni suM b+ha badzra ni hUM phaT/oM kArma rA ge oM badzra hUM badzre oM sarba gA ma NI oM hrIHoM A kA ra mu khe oM
8b2(rgyud): pradz+nyA pA ra mi te oM a m+wa hUM/oM oM sarba ta thA ga ta kA ya agre/wag badzre/oM sarba ta thA ga ga ta tsi t+ta badzre aHoM s+pho Ta ya sarba a bA ya ban+d+ha nA ne pra mo k+Sha ya sarba a pa ya ga ti b+h+yaHsarbas ma ya badzra hUM phaT//sdig pa dral ba'i 
8b3(rgyud): phyag rgya dang sngags so//de brjod pa tsam gyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsang ba mchog gi dngos grub rdo rje 'dzin pa chen po 'am de de bzhin gshegs pa nyid du 'gyur ro//'jam dpal 'di ni dge ba'i rtsa ba ma bskrun pa'i
8b4(rgyud): sems can rnams kyi rna lam du grag par mi 'gyur zhing dge ba'i rtsa ba ma bskrus pa'i sems can gyis bri ba dang klag pa dang /rjod pa dang rim gror bya ba dang bla mar bya ba dang /rjod par bya ba dang mchod pa bya bar yang nus pa ma yin no//
8b5(rgyud): gzhan du na gang gis shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul gyi sgo'i chos kyi rnam grangs 'di tha na yi ge gcig tsam yang thos nas sems can de sangs rgyas mang po la dge ba'i rtsa ba bskrun par rig par bya na slar yang mtha' dag
8b6(rgyud): ma lus pa rdzogs pa lta ci smos//'jam dpal sems can gang la la gang ga'i klung gi bye ma brgyad bcu snyed kyi sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig la phyag byas bla mar byas rjed par byas mchod par byas na des shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin
8b7(rgyud): pa'i tshul 'di nyan par nus so//gang du chos kyi rnam grangs 'di rab du spyod par 'gyur pa'i sa'i phyogs de mchod rten du gyur par 'gyur te gang zag de yang phyag bya ba'i 'os su 'gyur ro//gang chos kyi rnam grangs 'di yid la bzung ba
8b8(rgyud): 'am/glegs bam du bris bar gyur na de bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong mang por skye ba dran par 'gyur ro//de la bdud sdig to can gyis bar du mi gcod do//rgyal po chen po bzhi po rnams dang /gzhan yang lha rnams kyis phyi 
9a1(rgyud): @#//bzhin rjes su 'brangs te/de bsrung ba dang skyob pa dang/sbed pa byed par 'gyur zhing tshul ma yin pas dus byed par mi 'gyur la sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis dgongs par
9a2(rgyud): 'gyur ro//mdor na sangs rgyas kyi zhing gang dang gang yid la 'dod pa de dang der skye bar 'gyur ro//'jam dpal 'di lta bu'i phan yon mang po 'di ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul gyi sgo'i chos kyi rnam grangs so//
9a3(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen pos rdo rje'i snying po thams cad kyi rgyan theg pa chen po mngon par rtogs pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mchog tu gsang ba chen po mchog tu gsang ba'i
9a4(rgyud): dam tshig rdo rje dbyings kyi dbang phyug dam pa chen po 'khor los sgyur pa chen po bzhis yongs su bskor ba rdo rje snying po rgyan ces bya ba'i snying po 'di phyung ngo//mtshan nyid gcig pa'i phyir chos thams cad gdod nas ma skyes
9a5(rgyud): pa'o||nam mkha' dang mnyam pa'i phyir chos thams cad stong pa nyid do//spros pa thams cad spangs pa'i phyir chos thams cad god ma nas yongs su dag pa'o//thams cad kyi sems gsal ba'i mtshan nyid kyi phyir
9a6(rgyud): chos thams cad chos kyi dbyings yongs su rgyas pa'o//aHb+h+ru~M trA~M hrIHhUM de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi rdo rje yi ge'i tshig 'di brjod pa tsam gyis chos kyi dbyings kyi mthas klas pa sangs rgyas kyi yul//
9a7(rgyud): thams cad de snyed nyid du shin tu gsal ba rgya chen pos snang bar gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis slar yang bsdu bas byon nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi dkyil 'khor
9a8(rgyud): chen po'i bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la bka' stsal pa/'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa thamasas cad kyi rdo rje snying po rgyan zhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor
9b1(rgyud): chen po bshad par bya yis nyon cig de legs par nyon la rab tu yid la gyis shig bshad par bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad chen pos 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la bka' stsal pa/'jam 
9b2(rgyud): dpal khyod kyang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi shes rab ye shes la zhugs la/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rdo rje'i snying po chen po la byin gyis brlabs par byos shig/de nas yang 'jam dpal gzhon nur
9b3(rgyud): gyur pas bcom ldan 'das rnam pa snang mdzad kyis gsol pa btab pa'i tshig thos nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi thugs khar zhugs te/byin gyis brlabs nas de las 'thon te/de bzhin gshegs pa
9b4(rgyud): thams cad kyi spyan sngar rnam par gnas so//de nas bcom ldan 'da'as kyis de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi dam tshig 'byung gnas/rdo rje snying po'i rgyan byin gyis rlob pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par
9b5(rgyud): zhugs nas chos kyi dbyings kyi rdo rje chen po snying po'i rgyan de bzhin gshegs pa thams1 kyi gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor bkod pa chen po bka' stsal pa/de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//dkyil 'khor chen po mchog
9b6(rgyud): gyur pa//rdo rje snying po gsang chen po//de bzhin gshegs pa kun ldan dge/rdo rje snying po dang mtshungs pa//khams rnams thams cad rnam par sprul//sangs rgyas kun gyi bdud rtsi dang //gzhan yang bcud len dang
9b7(rgyud): mtshungs pa//de bzhin gshegs pa kum gyi gsang //rdo rje kun gyi rang bzhin grong //gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//rta babs bzhi ni rnam par mdzes//thig bzhi dang ni yang dag ldan//dar dang me tog chun pos spros//
9b8(rgyud): grwa yi cha rnams thams cad dang //sgo khyud kyi ni mtshams rnams su//rdo rje rin chen rnams kyis spras//rdo rje'i thig rnams kyis spras pa'i//phyi rol dkyil 'khor thig gdab bya//de yi'ang phyi rol du bya ba//
10a1(rgyud): dkyil 'khor bdud rtsi yi ni 'od//khyad par ji lta ba bzhin du//sngags pas kun nas yongs bskor bya//de ltar rnam bzhi'i rnam pa dang //ji srid grong ni rnam pa bdun//sbyor ba bdun dang yang dag ldan//
10a2(rgyud): ngar dang me tog phreng bas brgyan//sgo dang sgo khyud rin chen ni//yang dag mtho ba legs par gnas//de yi grong gi nang du ni//rab zhugs 'khor lo 'dab ru//rdo rje thig gis yongs su bskor//ka ba brgyad kyis nye 
10a3(rgyud): bar ma nges//rdo rje ka mdun gnas rnams su//dkyil 'khor lnga yis brgyan pa'o//de ltar 'khor lo rnam pa gnyis//nang gi dkyil 'khor dang mtshungs pa//tshad mdzes pa yi snying po tshe//rdo rje 'khor los rnam par 
10a4(rgyud): brgyan//gru bzhi pa dang zlum po nyid//gru gsum rnam par yang dag byung //zla phyed rnam pa dang mtshungs pa//dkyil 'khor rnam pa bzhi slar yang //slob dpon rdo rje yang dag 'byung //shes rab chen pos rab brtag bya//dbus 
10a5(rgyud): kyi dkyil 'khor dbus su ni//pad ma chen po 'dab brgyad pa//pad ma rA ga'i 'od mtshungs la//sangs rgyas gzugs brnyan gzhag par bya//dkyil 'khor gyi ni dbus nyid du//sangs rgyas thams cad bsdus pa'o//
10a6(rgyud): sangs rgyas kyi ni ngos kun du//shes rab can gyis gzhag bya ba//dam tshig mchog bzhi rnam bzhi ni//go rim bzhin du yang dag bri//rdo rje chen po seng ge'i khrir//'khor lo can gyi khyab bdag gzhag/gdugs 
10a7(rgyud): dkar rgyal ba'i gtsug tor dang //gzi brjid phung po skal pa che//rnam par 'phro byed rnam rgyal nyid//de bzhin gtsug tor lnga po ni//sangs rgyas skyob pa thams cad la//yongs su bskor nas snam bu la//dkyil 
10a8(rgyud): 'khor kun tu bri bar bya//rdo rje shugs kyis song nas ni//dkyil 'khor dag ni bzhi po ru//mi bskyod la sogs rnam pa bzhi//rdo rje rnal 'byor dbang phyug bri//mi bskyod pa yi dkyil 'khor ni//zla 'od lta bu'i mdog tu bya 
10b1(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs rgyal//kun du tshogs kyi dbang phyug rnams//rdo rje slob dpon stobs po che//shes rab chen pos dgod par bya//rin chen 'byung gnas dkyil 'khor du//rdo rje rin chen sogs kyis dgang //rdo rje chos sogs 
10b2(rgyud): gsang ba yi//dkyil 'khor tshe dpag med pa'o//rdo rje las la sogs ldan pa//don yod grub pa'i dkyil 'khor ro//gnyis pa 'khor lo'i dkyil 'khor du//rdo rje shugs kyis song nas ni//rnam gzigs la sogs 
10b3(rgyud): sangs rgyas rnams//dkyil 'khor kun du der ni bri//rdo rje snying po rgyal po che//khyab bdag gsang ba'i chos dbang phyug/'dod pa'i don kun grub pa'i phyir//'bad pa yis ni bri bar bya//blo gros rgya mtsho mi zad blo//byams pa bdag 
10b4(rgyud): nyid chen po dang //nam mkha'i snying po grags chen po//kun du bzang po nyid dag dang //phyag na rdo rje bdag nyid che//kun nas 'bar bas 'khrugs pa'i 'od//'jam pa'i dbyangs ni rigs kyi mchog/sangs rgyas sras bzang 
10b5(rgyud): dri ma med//sgrib pa thams cad nges par sel//de nas sa yi snying po mchog/nam mkha'i mdzod ni mdzod dam pa//lag na rin chen bla na med//ngan song kun ni 'doms pa ni//dra ba can gyi 'od ces bya//dpa' bo 'di dag 
10b6(rgyud): gsang ba che//dkyil 'khor 'di dag bri bar bya//'khor lo de ni dor nas ni//blo gros ldan pas phyir song ste//shar du sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//phyag rgya go rims bzhin du dgod//'khor lo mdzes pa'i dge legs can//
10b7(rgyud): rdo rje dpal be'u pad ces bya//gtsug tor rnam pa sna tshogs pa//rdo rje pad mas rnam par brgyan//lhung bzed dang ni khar sil dang //chos gos kyang ni khyad par du//shAkya seng ge ji srid par//sangs rgyas kun gyis rab tu 
10b8(rgyud): gsungs//gang zhig dngos grub mchog 'dod pas//de rnams blo ldan brtan pas bri//byang du 'jig rten dbang phyug gi/phyag rgya'i tshogs mchog de bzhin te//pad ma ud pal nyid dang ni//spyi blugs lpags pa'i 
11a1(rgyud): @#//g.yang gzhi dang //dbyug gu'i shing ni mkhas pa yis//ma myos pa ru bri bar bya//slar yang lho ru song nas ni//phyag na rdo rje bdag nyid che//slob dpon bdag nyid chen po yis//phyag rgya 'bad pas bri bar bya//
11a2(rgyud): rdo rje paN+Ti sha dang ni//dril bu rab tu mdzes pa dang //dbyug gu dang ni tho ba dang //dbyug pa rdo rje mtshon pa dang //lcags sgrog dang ni rtse gsum yang //'di dag dpa' bo'i phyag rgya yin//dkyil 'khor nub du song nas ni//'jam 
11a3(rgyud): dpal dbyangs ni blo dang ldan//gsang ba'i chos ni dam pa'i mchog/brtson 'grus chen po'i phyag rgya bri//ud pal 'khor lo rdo rje dang //ral gri yang ni khyad par du//thod pa dang ni dgra sta nyid//rma bya dang ni seng ge nyid//phyag rgya 'di 
11a4(rgyud): ni brtson 'grus che//mkhas pa yis ni dgod par bya//'khor lo'i grwa ni legs gnas par//rdo rje sgeg mo la sogs bri//phyi yi 'khor lo'i grwa ru ni//rdo rje bdug pa zhes bya sogs//bdag nyid chen po 'bad pa yis//mchod pa 'di dag 
11a5(rgyud): bri bar bya//phyi yi dkyil 'khor grwa rnams su//gzugs kyi rdo rje la sogs min//gzugs sgra de bzhin dri dang ni//khyad par du ni ro nyid dang //reg dang chos kyi dbyings dang ni//mkhas pa yis ni dgodapar bya//sgo yi nang ni thams cad 
11a6(rgyud): du//sgo skyong pa ni dgod par bya//de bzhin dkyil 'khor gnyis pa ru//rdo rje shugs kyis song nas ni//go rims bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi//yum rnams 'bad pas bri bar bya//nam mkha'i spyan zhes bya ba ni//sangs rgyas 
11a7(rgyud): spyan zhes bya ba dang //shes rab pha rol phyin ma yang //phyag rgya kun dang yang dag ldan//ji srid de bzhin gshegs rig ma//thams cad shar du dgod par bya//de nas blo gros ldan pa yis//byang gi phyogs 
11a8(rgyud): su song nas ni//gos dkar mo dang sgrol ma che//khro gnyer can dang grags 'dzin ma//khyad par du ni dpal bdun dang //mda' bdun dang ni dbyangs can ma//de la sogs pa'i lha mo rnams//
11b1(rgyud): bran dang bran mor yang dag ldan//de bzhin pho nya pho nya mo//gsang ba'i phyag rgya lhan cig bri//rdo rje shugs kyis song nas ni//lho yi phyogs su rab phyin la//mA ma kI dang lcags sgrog dang //khab rtse rdo rje'i mtshan ma mchog/
11b2(rgyud): de ltar bris nas blo ldan gyis//nub kyi phyogs su song nas ni//der ni phyin nas shes ldan gyis//skra can stobs chen bri bar bya//de bzhin 'dam bu 'dar rgyud dang //lha mo zla mdzes 'od zer 'dra//dkyil 'khor kun gyi 
11b3(rgyud): mtshams rnams su//de bzhin nye ba'i skra can ma//'bad pa yis ni bri bar bya//rab bkra nor gyi blo gros dang //'gugs byed pho nya lnga dang ldan//grwa yi cha ru bri bya ba//rdo rje mdung rtse zhes bya sogs//sgo yi nang na thams cad 
11b4(rgyud): du//sgo skyong pa ni rnam par bri//gzhan gyis mi thub lha mo che//de bzhin ral pa gcig ma mchog/rdo rje dregs mo lha mo che//rdo rje kum b+hi yang dag bri//de nas blo ldan dkyil 'khor ni//thams cad las ni 'thon nas su//
11b5(rgyud): shar gyi cha ni thams cad du//sngags pa mnyam par gzhag pa yis//khro chen gzhan gyis mi thub pa//de bzhin mi g.yo zhes bya dang //khams gsum rnam rgyal snying po ni//mnga' bdag brgya phrag lnga yis 
11b6(rgyud): bskor//shar du bris nas sngags pa yis//de nas byang gi char song ste//rta mgrin khro bo chen po dang //skar ma'i spyan ni khyad par du'o//khro gnyer rim pa rnam bsgyings pa//khro bo blo ldan brtson 
11b7(rgyud): 'grus che//pho nya bran rnams thams cad dang //ma mo stong gis yongs skor bar//'jig rten mgon gyis bka' stsal to//de nas blo gros ldan pa yis//lho yi phyogs su song nas ni//der ni phyin te brtson chen po//
11b8(rgyud): khro bo chen po stobs po che//rdo rje mtshan pas legs mtshan pa//shes rab chen pos dgod par bya//khro bo zla ba'i thig le dang //de bzhin khro chen rdo rje 'chang //khro bo gzigs ma stobs po che//khro bo bdud rtsi 'khyil ba dang //
12a1(rgyud): @#//
12b1(rgyud): lus ngan du lus ngan rnams dang /phyi rol tu 'khor yug chen po'i ri'i rgyal po bri bar bya'o//rab dga'i nang du rgya mtsho chen po bdun dgod par bya'o//phyi rol gyi shar phyogs su khro bo gzhan gyis mi thub pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya chen po dgod par bya/lhor rdo rje 'jigs byed kyi spyan gyi phyag rgya chen po dgod pa bya'o//nub tu khro bo'i rgyal ba 
12b2(rgyud): chen po ga sha ni rje ga shad kyi phyag rgya chen po dgod parabya'o//byang du rta mgrin gyi phyag rgya chen po rnam par dgod par bya'o//de lta bu'i gru bzhi pa/sgo bzhi pa'i dkyil 'khor chen po dar dang me tog gi chun po dang /sgo 
12b3(rgyud): khyud dang /gdugs dang rgyal mtshan dang /ba dan bsgreng ba/dra ba dang dra ba phyed pas klubs pa/de lta bu'i dkyil 'khor chen po bris la bsgom par bya'o//de'i phyi rol du 'gro ba lnga po gzhag par bya'o//de bzhin du dbus

18b1(rgyud): pa'i 'od//dpral ba'i phyogs su gnas pa bsgom//de yis ngar thod dbang skur thob//rdo rje gzi brjid phyag rgya che//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor mtshungs pa'i 'od//snying khar gnas pa bsgom par bya//kun mkhyen ye shes ster bar byed//rin chen 
18b2(rgyud): tog gi phyag rgya che//legs par mnyam par gzhag pas bsgom//sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin pa mchog/sgrub po dam pas rdzogs par byed//rdo rje bzhad pa phyag rgya che//legs par gnyam par gzhag pas bsgoms//rdo rje bzhad pa ltar 
18b3(rgyud): gyur te//de ni skye dgu dga' byed 'gyur//dbang skur phyag rgya'i ye shes so//pad ma bde chen pad ma la//dag pa'i chos ni mnyam gzhag pas//dam pa'i don du bsgom par bya//mnyam med rdo rje chos dang mtshungs//shes rab 
18b4(rgyud): ye shes rdo rje rno//bsgoms pas de ni rdo rje 'chang //shes rab pha rol tu phyin dag pa//stong nyid sbyor bas bsgoms na ni//dam pa'i don du bsgoms bya de//mnyam med 'jam dbyangs dang ni mtshungs//rdo rje rgyu yi phyag rgya che//legs par

18b6(rgyud): chos kyi phyag rgya'i ye shes so//rdo rje las kyi phyag rgya che//dam pa'i dod du bsgoms na ni//kun nas kun nas rang nyid kyis//rdo rje las ni byed par 'gyur//rdo rje bsrung ba byams pa che//rdo rje chos zhes grags pa ste//chos kyi sbyor bas 
18b7(rgyud): bsgoms na ni//rdo rje bsrung ba dang mtshungs 'gyur//rdo rje gnod sbyin brtson 'grus che//khro bo chen po stobs po che//dam pa'i don gyis bsgom par bya//rdo rje gnod sbyin dang mtshungs 'gyur//rdo rji khu tshur phyag rgya che//lus ngag yid 
18b8(rgyud): kyis de bzhin du//rdo rje'i sbyor bas bsgoms na ni//rdo rje khu tshur 'dzin par 'gyur//las kyi phyag rgya'i ye shes so//sgeg mos mngon par dga' ba ster//phreng ba yang ni dbang bskur ba//glu mas mdzes pa'i sgra dbyangs byed//
19a1(rgyud): @#//gar mas rdzu 'phrul thob par 'gyur//khrims bzang po'i dri yin te//mo tog byang chub yan lag nyid//sgron mas mtha' dag gsal ba'o//bdug pas bdug pa dag la brtson//lcags kyus dbang phyug 'gugs 
19a2(rgyud): byed na||skye bo gzhan lta smos ci dgos//zhags pas dbang po 'ching 'gyur na//gdug pa'i sems can smos ci dgos//lcags sgrog gis ni 'phrog byed dang //tshangs sogs dbang phyug chen po 'di 'chad//'bebs pas 
19a3(rgyud): thams cad 'bebs 'gyur na//dman pa'i mi dag smos ci dgos//'di nyid kyis ni sbyor ba yis//chos kyi yi ge mchog gi ni//phyag rgya'i ye shes rab sbyong pas//sbyar te myur du bsgrub par bya//'di yi sbyor ba shes par bya//
19a4(rgyud): phyag rgya'i 'khor lo khyad par du//spyi gtsug snying kha rkang pa'i bar//yi ge yis ni yongs gang bar//gzugs brnyan gcig gi go 'phang gyur//sems rtse gcig gyur bsam gtan gyis//sa lar ba gnas pad ma ni//dbang 
19a5(rgyud): chen gyi ni gnas zhes bshad//dbang po gnyis ni mtshungs sbyor bas//dbang phyug kyang ni bsgrub par bya//dbang chen dag ni gru bzhi pa//dbang chen gyi ni las su bshad//chu ni zlum por shes par bya//pad ma'i mtshan mas rnam par 
19a6(rgyud): mtshan||zhi bar byed pa'i las dang ldan//zhi ba'i don du de bshad do//me ni gru gsum shes par bya//sum mdo yi ni gnas su gnas//dbang du bya ba bsgrub don du//pad ma rA ga 'dra ba'i 'od//mngon spyod la ni brtson 'grus che//rlung gi 
19a7(rgyud): dkyil 'khor brjod bya ste//ral gri yi ni phyag rgya'i dbyibs//dbyar gyi sprin dang mtshungs pa'i 'od//ser dang dkar dang de bzhin dmar//nag po yang ni khyad par du//dang po'i dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//tha ma'i bar du 
19a8(rgyud): rab tu brtag/dang po rdo rje rab tu bzhag/de yi rje sa su rin chen te//pad ma dang ni las rnal 'byor//grub pa'i go 'phang rim par bshad//phyag rgya thams cad kyi ye shes so////de nas bcom ldan 'das 
19b1(rgyud): rtag pas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi phyag rgya'i ye shes bka' stsal pa/gnyis dang gnyis su med pas yongs su dag pas chos thams cad rnam par grol ba'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//rtag pa dang chad pa yongs 
19b2(rgyud): su dag pas chos thams can zhi ba dang mtha' dang mtha' med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o|/zad pa'i sgo dang zad pa'i mtha' dang zad pa'i mur thug pa dang ldan pas chos thams cad zad pa dang 'dod chags dang bral ba'i phyag rgyas rgyas btabs pa'o//
19b3(rgyud): mnyam pa nyid kyi mtha' rnam par dag pas chos thams cad la skur ba mi 'debs pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab ba'o//mig lnga la yang dag par 'das pas chos thams cad nam mkha'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa 'o//sems can thams 
19b4(rgyud): cad nam mkha'i dbyings nyid kyi phyir chos thams cad nam mkha' rang bzhin gyis phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o////chos kyi dbyings su yang dag par 'du ba nyid kyi phyir/chos thams cad dbyer mi phyed pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btabapa pa'o//
19b5(rgyud): byang chub kyi sems dang tha mi dad pas chos thams cad byang chub kyi sems kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//chos kyi dbyings mi phyed pa'i mtshan nyid yin pas chos thams cad chos kyi dbyings kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab 
19b6(rgyud): pa'o//mad pas chos thams cad stong pa nyid kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab na'o//'tshe ba med pas chos thams cad bdag med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btabs pa'o//skad cig ma nyid pas chos thams cad mi rtag pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o/
19b7(rgyud): /dang 'chi ba'i mtha' dang ldan pas chos thams cad sdug bsngal gyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o/ /sngon gyi mtha' dang phyi ma'i mtha' de bzhin nyid pas chos thams cad de bzhin nyid kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btang ba'o//gdod pa nas yongs su dag 
19b8(rgyud): pas chos thams cad yang dag pa'i mtha'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa 'o//'dus byas dang 'dus ma byas mnyam pa nyid pas chos thams cad chen po stong pa nyid kyi phyag rgyas btab pa'o//spros pa'i dmigs pa rnam par bral bas chos 
20a1(rgyud): @#//thams cad mtshan ma med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//re ba thams cad las rnam par grol bas chos thams cad smon pa med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas pa btab pa'o//dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis mtshan nyid kyis ni 
20a2(rgyud): chos thams cad mi rtag pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab ba'o//phung po lnga yongs su 'dus pa'i mtshan nyid kyis ni chos thams cad sdug bsngal gyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//rang bzhin gyis bdag med pas chos thams cad 
20a3(rgyud): bdag med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas gtab pa'o//shin tu mi g.yo ba'i phyir chos thams cad zhi ba'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa 'o//don dam pa'i bden pa yang dag par 'du ba'i phyir chos thams cad bden pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btabapa'o//
20a4(rgyud): 'tsho ba rnam par mi gnas pa'i phyir chos thams cad dri ma med pa'i phyag rgyas rgya sa btab pa'o//mchog tu mthar phyin pa'i phyir chos thams cad 'brug pa med pa'i phyag rgyas rgya btab pa'o//sngon gyi mtha' dang phyi ma'i mtha' 
20a5(rgyud): bcad pa'i phyir chos thams cadaji lta ba bzhin gyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab ba'o'ajal ba'i dbang du byed pa dang dbang du bya ba med pa'i phyir chos thams can sgro 'dogs pa med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o //byed pa pos byas pa'i
20a6(rgyud): phyir chos thams cad 'jig pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btababa'o//rang bzhin gyis ma byung ba'i phyir chos thams cada'abyung ba med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas gdab pa'o//rang bzhin gyis maskyes pa'i phyir chos thams cang 'gog pa med
20a7(rgyud): pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab ba'o//'gag pa med pa'i phyir chos thamasacad gdod ma nas skye ba med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyasabatab pa'o//rtog ge kun tu spyod pa thams cad dang bral ba'i phyir chos thams cad spros pa med pa'i phyag
20a8(rgyud): rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//gzugs rnam par lta ba med pa'i phyir chos tha mas cad mtshan nyid med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas pa btab pa'o//gnas yongs su bcad pa'i phyir chos thams cad nye bar sbyor ba med pa'i phyag rgyas gtab
20b1(rgyud): pa'o//mi mthun pa'i phyogs nye bar mi dmigs pa'i phyir chos thams cad la rjes su sbyor ba med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//mngon par 'du byed pa thams cad med pa'i phyir chos thams cad kyi rnam par smin pa med pa'i phyag 
20b2(rgyud): rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//skye ba dang gnas pa las rnam par grol ba'i phyir chos thams cad 'dus ma byas pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab ba'o//nam mkha' dang khyad par med pa'i phyir chos thams ces mnyam pa nyid kyi phyag rgyas rgyas 
20b3(rgyud): btab pa'o//mtshan nyid gcig pa'i phyir chos thams cad gzugs dang /tshor ba dang /'du shes dang 'du byed dang rnam par shes pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btabs pa'o//snying po med pa'i phyir chos thams cig phung po'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab 
20b4(rgyud): pa'o//snying po dang bcas pa med pa'i phyir chos thams chad khams dang skye mched kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o////sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sku dang mnyam pa nyid thob pas chos thams cad khams dang skye mched kyi 
20b5(rgyud): btab pa'o//mtha' gcig tu 'jig rten gyi lam rnam par dag pa'i phyir chos thams can 'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//lha'i rjes su byed pa'i phyir chos thams cad phyag rgya chen po'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab
20b6(rgyud): pa'o//mtha' gcig tu chos la phan 'dogs pa'i phyir chos thams cad chos kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab ba'o//thams cad kyi las sna tshogs byed pa'i phyir chos thams ces las kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab
20b7(rgyud): ba'o//thams cad dam tshig thams cad rjes su bsgrub pa'i phyir chos thams cad dam tshig gi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o //sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad rab tu mi gnas pa'i phyir chos thams ca nas sangs rgyas kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab
21a1(rgyud): @#//
21b1(rgyud): mkhyen ye shes kyis gang ba//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi gsang //ra sA ya na dang mtshungs mchog/kha yig mkha' 'drar shes par bya//thig le nA das rnam par spras//gsang ba chen po mchog tu mdzes//thog ma tha ma med pa'i 
21b2(rgyud): bdag/de yi steng du 'od zer che//'bar ba'i phreng ba 'khrugs pa'i 'od//rdo rje rdo rje lta bur gnas//spyi bor rtse mo lnga dang ldan//de yi steng du rtag bsams pa//yi ge mchog gi go 'phang ngo //man ngag mchog las shes bya ste//gzhan
