21b8(rgyud): lngas ni dmigs nas yang //mi rnams lus ni dag par 'gyur//ji snyed bud med yon tan dang //ldan pa kun du gnas pa ni//bkug nas bcug ste bcings nas su//dbang du bya ste bsgrub par bya//de rnams lhan cig yongs rten nas//
22a1(rgyud): @#//sngags pa mchog gi dngos grub thob//gsang ba mchog tu dgyes pa na//shes rab pha rol phyin pa che//zhi ba'i chos kyi rim pa mchog/shin du mnyam par gzhag pa bsgom//rdo rje sems dpa'i dngos grub gang //de 
22a2(rgyud): 'grub gzhan lta smos ci dgos//mchog ni rtag par shes par bya//bar mas phun sum tshogs pa ste//stong pa nyid dang mtshan med mchog/smon pa med pa zhes su grags//chos kun bdag med pa yi mchog/kun mkhyen ye 
22a3(rgyud): shes rdzogs par byed//sa bcu yi ni stobs dag gis//pha rol phyin pa thams cad thob//rdo rje sems dpa' mchog bsgoms na//rdo rje sems dpa' 'dra bar 'gyur//de nyid 'di ni bcud kyis len//gnyis su med pa'i ye 
22a4(rgyud): shes mchog/theg chen mchog tu yid 'ong ba//stong nyid bsgom pa'i bdag nyid can//gnyis su med pa'i yeshes mchog/gang gis rtag tu sgom byed na//de yang rdo rje 'dzin rgyal po//yon tan mchog las skyes par 'gyur//
22a5(rgyud): rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par rgyan//bud med lus ngag yid sbyor bas//yongs reg A li gtsum pas//mchog gi dngos grub thob par 'gyur//bud med kha dog ljang khu gang //rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam pa brgyan//de 
22a6(rgyud): tshe de ni sngags pas bsten//mnyam med gsang ba'i ye shes mtshungs//bud med gang zhig kha dog dkar//pad ma'i 'dab ma 'dra ba'i 'od//de dang lhan cig sngags pas bsdad//dam pa'i dngos grub thob par 'gyur /bu mo 
22a7(rgyud): gang zhig kha dog dmar//ud pa la yi rgyan 'chang ma//sngags pas de dang lhan cig gnas//shes rab pha rol phyin mtshungs 'gyur//bud med in tra nI la mtshungs//gsang ba rdo rje rjes su sbyor//rig 'dzin rnams kyi 
22a8(rgyud): rgyal po ni//stobs chen grub par de 'gyur ro//gsang chen ma raka ta mtshungs//gang gis rtag tu bsten pa ni//de yang rdo rje 'dzin rgyal po//myur du gsang ba'i dam par 'gyur//bud med pad ma rA ga 'dra//rgyan rnams 
22b1(rgyud): kun gyis rnam par brgyan//dbang po gnyis ni snyoms 'jug pas//rdo rje 'dzin ma lta bu bsgrub//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//gnyis su med pa'i ye shes mchog/bsam pas rtag tu bsgom pa ni//mnyam med 
22b2(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun dang mtshungs//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi//mchog gis gnas kyis bsgoms na ni//rnal 'byor mchog gis mi ring bar//rdo rje 'dzin pa lta bur 'gyur//yang dag spong ba rnam pa bzhi//legs par mnyam 
22b3(rgyud): par gzhag pa bsgom//dbang po gnyis ni snyom 'jug pas//mnyam med 'jig rten dbang phyug mtshungs//so sor yang dag rig pa bzhi//shin du mnyam bzhag bsgoms na ni//rdo rje sangs rgyas ting 'dzin mchog/mnyam med 
22b4(rgyud): 'jam pa'i mchog dang 'dra//mi 'jigs pa ni rnam pa bzhi//rdo rje'i sbyor bas bsgom par bya//dbang po gnyis ni snyoma'i 'jug pas//rdo rje 'dzinapa lta bu ste//rdzu 'phrul gyi ni rkang pa bzhi//shin du mnyam gzhag bsgom na ni//
22b5(rgyud): dbang po gnyis ni mnyam sbyor bas//rdzu 'phrul ldan pa'i mchog tu 'gyur//tshangs pa yi ni gnas bzhi po//tshad med pa yi sems kyis ni//rdo rje sbyar bar bdag bsgoms na//rdo rje 'dzin pa lta bu 'grub//sbyor ba'i bdag nyid 
22b6(rgyud): dbang po lnga//dam pa'i don gyis bsgoms na ni//dbang po gnyis ni snyems 'jug pas//mnyam med rdo rje sangs rgyas mtshungs//yid kyi stobs ni rnam pa lnga//shin tu mnyam gzhag bsgoms na ni//'khor los sgyur ba brtson 'grus 
22b7(rgyud): che//stobs 'dzin lta bu nyid du 'gyur//byang chub yan lag rin chen bdun//gsang sngags bsgom pas bsgoms na ni//rtog pa'i ye shes sbyor ba yis//khyab bdag chos kun 'dzin par 'gyur//'phags pa'i yan lag brgyad kyi lam//
22b8(rgyud): kun mkhyen ye shes rdzogs byed pa//de ni bsgoms pa tsam gyis ni//'jig rten shes la rab tu 'da'//rjes su dran pa rnam pa drug/bdag nyid rtse gcig gis bsgoms na//sngags pa rdo rje 'dzin pa ru//sngags pa dus mi ring par 'gyur//
23a1(rgyud): stobs bcu po ni yang dag bsgoms//bsgom pa'i rnal 'byor de nyid kyis//bsod nams ye shes yongs rdzogs te//rtag tu stobs bcu'i dam par 'gyur//pha rol phyin bcu yongs su rdzogs//stobs rnams dang ni 
23a2(rgyud): dbang rnams dang //sa rnams kun ni des bskyed nas//myur du mtshan ma dag par 'gyur//rnam grol gos kyis bsgribs nas su//de yi nu ma shin du bsnun//rje dar gyis kyang de bzhin du//mchu yis mchu ni yongs su gzung //'bad pas 
23a3(rgyud): dam du a ling ga/byang chub sems ni bsgom par bya//rdo rje ye shes brtson 'grus che//rdo rje dbyings su bdag brtan pas//rnam pa sna tshogs ka ra na//rnal 'byor shes pa de bsten bya//bud med rnams kyi dam tshig 'grub//rdo rje sems 
23a4(rgyud): dpa' mtshungs par 'gyur//bud med rnams kyi b+ha gar bsnun//ling ga myur du gang bar 'gyur//sbyor ba 'di ni mkhyen pa yis//stobs chen sangs rgyas rab tu grub//gsang ba 'di ni dam pa ste//sangs rgyas kun gyi skyob pa'o//
23a5(rgyud): gsang ba 'di yi sbyor ba yis//yi ge'i dam pa 'grub par 'gyur//sangs rgyas rnams kyi ma 'dres pa'i//chos su grags pa gang yin gang rnams sgom byed gtso bo ni//dngos grub thams cad rab tshogs pa'o//ma 'dres pa yi chos 
23a6(rgyud): bcwa brgyad//sangs rgyas chos su grags pa ste//de rnams bsgom pa dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas byang chub rab ster byed//bsam gtan bzhi po thob nas ni//gzugs med pa yi rtag par yang //'gog pa'i snyoms par 'jug ldan 
23a7(rgyud): pas//sangs rgyas byang chubs bsgrub pa po//byang chub phyogs ni sum bcu bdun//chos ni mchog tu rmad byung ba//de rnams bsgom pa dang/ldan pas//byang chub sems dpa'i skyob pa'o//sngags rnams thams cad rab 'grub cing /sbyor ba 
23a8(rgyud): thams cad thams cad du'o//des na 'bad pa thams cad kyis//sangs rgyas chos nyid bsgom par bya//'jig rten 'jig rten 'das pa'i lam//ye shes rin chen rab rnyed dka'//mchod dang dbang bskur byin brlabs pas//
23b1(rgyud): rnyed 'gyur gzhan du ma yin no//bshags dang gsol ba gdab pa dang //bdag nyid dbul ba de bzhin no//sangs rgyas sogs la yi rang zhing //de bzhin yongs su bsngo ba dang //byang chub sems ni yang dag bskyed//
23b2(rgyud): khyad par du ni dus kun du'o//thog ma bar dang phye ma red du//ji lta bzhin du rjes bsgoms pas//sangs rgyas thams cad bsgrub bya ste//'di la the tshom yod ma yin//'gro ba sems kyis spro ba ni//brtan pa'i snying por legs par 
23b3(rgyud): bshad//stong nyid de bzhin nyid las byung //rdo rje sems dpar de bshad do//'gro ba thams cad bkug nas su//sangs rgyas byang chub sbyar bar bya//don yod ye shes dag pa de//rdo rje rgyal po mchog ces bshad//rdo rje chags pas 
23b4(rgyud): chags gyur nas//rang bzhin gyis ni brtan par 'gyur//'gro ba ma lus lus med pa//de yi sems ni chags par bshad//chos rnams kun ni rang bzhin gyis//rnam par dben zhing gnyis su med//gang tshe dag pa'i bdag 
23b5(rgyud): nyid mthong //de tshe rdo rje legs par bshad//rdo rje sems dpa' bzhi po yis//ming dang brda ni sbyar ba'o//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//sdom pa mchog tu dam pa'o//khams gsum pa yi rgyal srid che//tshogs 
23b6(rgyud): gnyis rdzogs par byas pa ste//sa dang pha rol phyin mchog gtso//rdo rje dbang bskur las byung ba//de bzhin gshegs kun ye shes te//rnyog pa med cing yi ge med//'od kyi dkyil 'khor gzi brjid mchog/brtan pa rtag pa nyid 
23b7(rgyud): du 'gyur//nam mkha'i mdzod ni mdzod chen po//chos rnams thams cad dbang bsgyur ba//yid bzhin nor bu rdo rje che//rdo rje tog ni bla na med//ye shes snang ba rin chen che//rmad du byung ba nges par mthong //de bzhin gshegs kun gsang 
23b8(rgyud): ba ni//rdo rje bzhad pa zhes su grags//chos kyi bdag med las byung ba//gnyis su med pa'i ye shes mchog/chos nyid dam pa bsam du med//rdo rje chos zhes bya bar dran//rdo rje blo gros snang ba che//shes rab ye shes dri med mchog/
24a1(rgyud): @#//zag med shes rab chen po pa//de ni rdo rje rnon por bshad//sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos 'khor lo//gnyis su med las byung bar mtshon//yan lag bcu gnyis rten 'brel ming //rdo rje rgyu zhes nges par bshad//dgongs pa'i skad 
24a2(rgyud): ni spros pa'i mchog/yul rnams kyis ni bshad pa ste//sangs rgyas kun gyis bzlas pa che//de ni rdo rje smra bar bshad//sems can kun gyi don la sbyar//sangs rgyas kun gyi mchod pa che//gdong nas dag pa'i rnal 'byor che//de ni rdo rje las 
24a3(rgyud): su bshad//bla na med pa'i byams pa che//bsrung ba'i bya bgong na med//dag pa'i chos ni brtson chen po//rdo rje bsrung ba zhes su grags//shes rab thabs kyi khro bo che//sangs rgyas kun dang rjes 'thun pa//nyon mongs thams cad zab che//
24a4(rgyud): de ni rdo rje gnod sbyin brjod//lus ngag yid ni yang dag sbyor//sgrib pa med cing yi ge bral//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyis gsang //de ni rdo rje mtshams sbyor bshad//rdo rje ye shes rnal 'byor che//sangs rgyas kun gyi stobs po che//
24a5(rgyud): thog ma tha ma med zhi ba//rig pa mchog ces bya bar bshad//de nas yang 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje'i glus de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad legs par mchod nas glur blangs pa//oM 
24a6(rgyud): sarba bud d+ha ba ra me shwa ra ba d+d+h+ya shu d+d+h+ya la li hoHsu b+ha gaHsu ra taHma hA ma hA A di ma hA bud+d+ha bud+d+ha yo gI shwa ra ta thA tA b+hA bi twad ma ka ta thA ga ta/byang chub sems bskyed las 'khrungs pa//sangs rgyas kun la phyag 'tshal lo//
24a7(rgyud): mchog gi dbang phyug dam pa'i chos//gdod nas zhi ba la phyag 'tshal//byang chub sems dpa' skyob pa po//rdo rje can kun la phyag 'tshal//de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje che//sangs rgyas byang chub la phyag 'tshal//mi dmigs go 
24a8(rgyud): 'phang la gnas pa//mtha' las 'das pa la phyag 'tshal//'dren pa rnams kyi stobs po che//hU~M gi yi ge ma lus pa//thams cad mkhyen pa mtha' dag dang //shin du yongs su mya ngan 'da'//'jig rten dbang phyug mgon 
24b1(rgyud): bzang po//rdo rje ye shes dri ma med//rnam par dag pa rdo rje mchog/dngos grub thams cad rab gsol byed//rnal 'byor dbang phyug rnams kyi mgon//dus gsum bsam pa dbang sgyur ba//sems can rnams la phan don du//'byung ba khyod 
24b2(rgyud): la phyag 'tshal 'dud//rdo rje snying po rgyan las dkyil 'khor chen po yongs su rdzogs so////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams slar yang 'dus par gyur nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs khar 
24b3(rgyud): zhugs te bcom ldan 'das rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen po rdo rje snying po'i rgyan gyi dkyil 'khor chen po'i phyag rgya'i don ched du brjod pa/de nas yang dag bshad bya ba/dkyil 'khor chen po dam pa ste//rdo rje snying po zhes grags pa//sangs rgyas 
24b4(rgyud): kun gyi skyob pa'o//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//rta babs bzhi ni rnam par mdzes//sgo khyud rdo rje'i gzugs brnyan dang //phyag rgya dang ldan rab tu mdzes//dkyil 'khor de yi dbus su ni//'khor lo gnyis ni rab tu gnas//rtsibs 
24b5(rgyud): brgyad pa dang bcu drug rtsibs//rnam par dbye ba legs par brtag/dbus kyi dkyil 'khor nang gi ni//lte ba'i gnas su legs brtags pas//pad ma chen po mdab brgyad pa//ge sar gtams pas rab tu mdzes//de yi lte ba'i dbus 
24b6(rgyud): su ni//khyab bdag rnal 'byor dbang phyug che//rnam par snang mdzad brtson chen po//sems can kun la rjes brtse bas//de yi nye 'khor kun du ni//de bzhin gshegs pa brgyad rnams dgod//shar du dgod bya 'khor lo can//gtsug 
24b7(rgyud): tor dkar po nub tu gnas//gzi brjid phung po lho ru'o//rnam par rgyal ba byang du gnas//dbang ldan rnam par gsal bar bri//mer ni steng 'gro dgod par bya//steng 'gro chen po bden bral du//rlung du rgyal ba chen po gnas//sangs 
24b8(rgyud): rgyas gzugs brnyan 'dzin pa'i mchog/rnal 'byor ldan pa 'di dag bri//mi bskyod pa ni shar du dgod//lho ru rin chen 'byung ldan no//nub tu 'od dpag med par 'gyur//byang du don yod grub pa'i ming //zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la 
25a1(rgyud): @#//brten pa'o//dbang ldan phyogs nas brtsams nas ni//rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs bri//thams cad zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gnas//'khor rnams zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la'o//phreng ba rnam par dgod bya ba//rang gi phyag rgya 'od 
25a2(rgyud): zer 'bar//rdo rje sgeg mo la sogs pa'i//lha mo 'bad pas dgod par bya//rdo rje bdug pa la sogs bri//de bzhin gzugs kyi rdo rje sogs//rdo rje lcags kyu la sogs pa//sgo bsrungs 'bad pas bri bar bya//phyi yi dkyil 'khor la gnas 
25a3(rgyud): par//kun du bzang sogs rim bzhin bri//rang gi phyag rgya'i 'khor rnams dang //pho nya yi ni 'khor dag dang //khro bo rnams dang ma dag pa dang //de bzhin pho nya pho nya ma//rgya mtsho yi ni dkyil 'khor ni//'od kyi dkyil 'khor gsal ba'o//
25a4(rgyud): phyi ru phyi rol rdo rje'i ming //dkyil 'khor la ni 'bad pas bri//dbang ldan phyogs nas brtsams nas su//'jig rten skyong ba brgyad po bri//tshangs pa khyab 'jug dbang chen ming //dbang po la sogs lha thams cad//ma mo kun dang sring mo rnams//
25a5(rgyud): rgyal po chen po bzhi dang klu//de bzhin lha rnams thams cad dang //brtson 'grus chen po nam mkha' lding //'bad pa yis ni bri bar bya//de lta'i dkyil 'khor bris nas su//khyad par du ni mchod par bya//me tog bdug spos rol mo sogs//
25a6(rgyud): de bzhin bza' dang ston mo sogs//phreng ba'i rgyan ni sna tshogs kyis//de bzhin gshegs rnams mchod par bya//dkyil 'khor kun gyi cho ga yis//mchod pa kun gyis thams cad nas//'khor lo kun gyi cho ga yis//sangs rgyas 
25a7(rgyud): thams cad mchod par bya//de nas slob ma thams cad gzhug/yongs su dag pa khyad par du//byang chub sems ni bskyed nas su//byang chub bslab pa rjes spyod pa//cho ga shes pa'i sngags pas gzhug/'dren pa rnams la mchod 
25a8(rgyud): du gzhug/rdo rje'i dbang bskur sbyor ba yis//dbang bskur ba ni de nas sbyin//seng ge'i khri yi steng du ni//dar dang me tog chun pos spras//dkar po'i gdugs dang rin chen che//bla re rgya che 'bar ba dang //de yi g.yas kyi drung du ni//
25b1(rgyud): mkhas pa yis ni gzhag par bya//ba dan rnam pa sna tshogs dang //sna tshogs ras kyis rab mchod pa//g.yon gyi drung du thams cad nas//'bad pa yis ni gzhag par bya//rin chen sman kun gyis gang ba'i//bum pa 
25b2(rgyud): legs par gzhag byas te//rdo rje ming gi dbang bskur gyis//de la rang gis dbang bskur bya//rdo rje ming gis dbang bskur ba//mkhas pa yis ni slar yang bya//kun gyi cod pan dbang bskur gyis//yang dang yang du dbang bskur bya//chos kyi cod pan dbang bskur dang //sangs 
25b3(rgyud): rgyas kun gyi skyob pa ni//sna tshogs las kyi dbang bskur sbyin//chos kyi dbyings dang mnyam mtshungs pa'o//chos kyi sku yi dbang bskur ba//sangs rgyas kun gyi sgron ma'i sbyin//de ltar slob ma dbang bskur ba//khyad par du ni sngags pas bya//de la 
25b4(rgyud): dam tshig cho ga yis//sngags dang phyag rgyas sbyar bar bya//slob dpon brnyas par mi bya zhing //sgrub po kun la smad mi bya//sngags kyi spyod pa thams cad la//'da' bar mi bya smad mi bya//'jig rten pa yi lha rnams dang //grub mtha'i rigs 
25b5(rgyud): pa thams cad la//smad par mi bya blang mi bya//de la mngon par dga' ba spangs//sdig pa thams cad rnam spang zhing //de bzhin bsod nams thams cad bsdu//bza' dang ston mo du ma la//bza' bar mi bya smad mi bya/'dod pas bcom ldan 
25b6(rgyud): sems dpa' ste//sangs rgyas kun gyi sras bdag skyes//thog ma tha ma med zhi ba//nam yang dor bar mi bya'o//sangs rgyas thams cad chags las 'khrungs//de bzhin zhe sdang rmongs las 'khrungs//mnyam dang mi mnyam chos kun dang //
25b7(rgyud): dbu ma'i gnas la gnas nas su//de nyid dam tshig shes nas su//sangs rgyas byang chub yongs mi spong //lus can kun la 'tshe mi bya//khyod kyis ser sna mi bya ste//phrag dog bya bar mi bya'o//dam pa'i chos ni spang mi bya//byang chub sems 
25b8(rgyud): ni mchog tu bde//chang gi btung ba spang bya ste//pha rol bud med rnam par spang //thams cad mkhyen pa yongs mi dor//rtag tu srog chags gsad mi bya//sems can kun la snying rje dang //byams pa yang ni rnam par bsgom//sems 
26a1(rgyud): @#//
26b1(rgyud): lcags kyu mda' yi sbyor ba yis//legs so ba ni sbyar bar bya gung mo rdo rje rin chen rtse//phye ba gzi brjid mchog gi mchog/rgyal mtshan gnas su nges par sbyar//bzhad pa'i gnas su sbyar bar bya//rdo rje chos kyi phyag rgya che//dbu ma pad ma lta 
26b2(rgyud): bur bskum//ral gri 'dzin pa 'jam pa'i dbyangs//'khor lo sbyar ba'i 'khor los sgyur//kha ru phye ba'i phyag rgya che//rdo rje smra ba nyid du 'gyur//las kyi rdo rje'i phyag rgya che//gar mtshungs rdo rje sna tshogs can//chos yongs sdud pa'i 
26b3(rgyud): sbyor ba yis//rdo rje bsrung bar brtag par bya//mche ba gnyis ni rnam par gtsigs//rdo rje gnod sbyin zhes su bshad//khu tshur bcings pa khu tshur gyi//rdo rje thal mo snying khar gzhag/rdo rje sgeg mo zhes su grags//
26b4(rgyud): yongs su sbyar ba spyi bor gzhag/rdo rje phreng ba zhes su brjod//thal mo sbyar ba gdong du dgrol//rdo rje glu mar rjes su bsgrags//'gyed cing 'dud pa'i sbyor ba yi//de ni rdo rje gar mar bshad//pad ma'i thal mo gdong du dbye//spyi bor 
26b5(rgyud): yongs su sbyar la gzhag/snying khar mthe bo bstams pa dang /rab tu brkyang ba byug pa'o//lag pa steng du yongs brkyang ba//gzugs kyi phyag rgya nyid du 'gyur//lag gnyis rna ba'i lam du gzhag/sgra yi phyag rgya nyid du bshad//dri ni 
26b6(rgyud): byug pa'i sbyor ba yis//dri yi phyag rgya zhes su brjod//gdong du snyim pa phye ba ni//ro yi phyag rgya zhes su bsgrags//mkha' la myur ba'i sbyor ba yis//reg pa reg pa zhes su bshad//mang du chung gnyis ni yongs su sbyar//
26b7(rgyud): mdzub mo lcags kyu'i sbyor ba las//de nyid zhags pa'i dbyibs dang ni//lu gu rgyud kyi rnam par brtag/bzhi pa rdo rje dril bu zhes//rdo rje 'bebs par rnam par brtag/phyag rgya kun dang nye ba'i rgya//blo gros ldan pa rang gis brtag/
26b8(rgyud): thal mo yongs su sbyar la bsdam//mdzub mo gnyis po bskum nas ni//rin po che yi rnam par bcing //kun du bzang po blo ldan gyis//de las rtse mo rtse mo bskum//sems kyis byams pa bsgoms nas so//mdzub mo sbyar la btsug 
27a1(rgyud): @#//nas ni//ral gri'i rnam par brtag pa ste//'di ni nam mkha'i snying po yi//phyag rgyar sangs rgyas kun gyis bsngags//de nas ral gri'i mtshon cha yi//rnam pa ru ni nyer gnas pa//phyag rgya 'di ni rnam 'gog par//thams cad kyis ni 
27a2(rgyud): yongs su brjod//gar ni sdud pa'i sbyor ba yis//rdo rje thal mo rab tu brgyad//pad ma'i lta bur rnam par brtag/pad ma'i lag pa blo ldan gyis//de nyid me tog zom tshul dang //ud pal rnam par ltar mdzes pa//'di ni pad ma sgrol ba'i rgya//pad ma'i 
27a3(rgyud): mgon po zhes su grags//mche ba gtsigs pa dang ldan pa'i//kha dang 'dra bar legs par brtag/'di ni rta mgrin phyag rgya ste//kha yi phyogs su rab tu brtag/pad ma'i thal mo dbral ba yi//khro gnyer du ni nye bar sbyar//rdo rje thal mo 'bebs pa 
27a4(rgyud): che//dpal gyi snying po rin chen ldan//yid bzhin nor bu'i rnam pa bzung //thal mo legs par rab tu brkyang //mi zad blo gros phyag rgya che//sangs rgyas kun gyis rab tu brtags//gar ni sdud pa'i sbyor pa yis//rin chen rdo rje snying khar gzhag/
27a5(rgyud): nam mkha' mdzod kyi phyag rgya ste//nam mkha'i mdzod nyid ster bar byed//rdo rje chen po legs par brkyang //mthe chung mthe bong nges par bcing //lag pa gnyis ni nges par brtag/blo gros rgya mtsho'i phyag rgyar 'gyur//rdo rje thal mo steng du 
27a6(rgyud): bsgreng //mdzub mo gnyis po sbyar bar bya//'di ni rdo rje snying po'i rgya//rdo rje snying pos bka' stsal to//khu tshur nang du byas nas su//mdzub mo gnyis po rtse mo bya//'di ni 'jam dpal phyag rgya ste//rdo rje ral gri zhes su brtag/de nyid shag ti'i 
27a7(rgyud): rnam pa ni//'jam pa'i dbyangs ni blo ldan gyi'o//gshin rje mthar byed mdud rtser 'gyur//mdud rtse chen por rab tu brtag/mdzub mo bskum par byas nas ni//gung mo'i steng du yongs su gzhag/zhags pa'i rnam par bldan pas//
27a8(rgyud): gshin rje yang ni 'jigs par byed//de nyid ral grir byas pa ni//skra can ma ru rab tu bshad//khu tshur thams cad nges bcings te//mdzub mo gcig ni bskum par bya//'di ni nye ba'i skra can rgyar//'jam pa'i dbyangs kyis 
27b1(rgyud): bka' stsal to//rdo rje bsdams pa steng bcings te//dbu ma gnyis ni rnam par spang //mdzub mo rdo rje'i rnam par bye//mthe'u chung dang ni mthe bong brkyang //'di ni rdo rje 'dzin chen po'i//phyag rgya don kun sgrub byed yin//mdzub mo mthe bos bsdams pa 
27b2(rgyud): ni//mA ma kIr ni yongs su bshad//mdzub mor lcags sgrog rnam pa las//gud mo'i dbu ma rdo rjer bya//'di ni rdo rje lcags sgrog rgya//rdo rje lta bur yongs su bshad//rdo rje chen po rtse lnga pa//khro bo gzegs mar rnam par bshad//mchog gi rtse 
27b3(rgyud): mo rtse gcig bya//rdo rje rtse mo nyid kyir bshad//lag na rin chen phyag rgya ni//lag gnyis legs par brkyang bar bya//sor mo pad ma'i dbyibs su bya//skul byed lha mor yongs su bshad//thal mo yongs su sbyar ba las//mdzub mo dang ni mthe 
27b4(rgyud): bong dag/mig gi tshul du sbyar bar bya//sangs rgyas spyan gyir rnam par bshad//bkra shis kyi ni dkyil dkrungs bca'//rnal 'byor rig pas lus bsrang ste//lag gnyis lhung bzed 'dzin 'dra ba//bsam gtan phyag rgyar rab tu bshad//
27b5(rgyud): 'di ni rnam par snang mdzad rgya//sangs rgyas kun gyi don mdzad pa'o//mi skyod pa yi sa la re ba//rin chen tog gi mchog sbyin bshad//bsam gtan phyag rgya 'od dpag med//ngo bo nyid kyi don rtogs byed//don yod gzigs pa'i phyag rgya ni//
27b6(rgyud): mi 'jigs 'jigs pa med pa sbyin//blo ldan rnal 'byor dkyil dkrungs gnas//sangs rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin bdag/mnyam par gzhag pa phyag rgya kun//'ching ba'am ni 'chang du gzhug/'jig rten pa yi phyag rgya la//lag pa gcig ni rab 
27b7(rgyud): brkyang ste//dbang ldan gyi ni rtse gsum pa//mthe bos mthe'u chung mnan par bya//lha yi dbang po'i rdo rjer 'gyur//lag pa g.yas pas brtag par bya//me yi oM yig lag pa dang //mthe bo gung mo nyid la gzhag/gshin rje'i rdo rjer dbyug 
27b8(rgyud): pa ste//mdzub mo dang ni mthe bong sbyar//de nyid khyab 'jug 'khor lo ste//'khor lo'i yi ge dgod par bya//lag pa gcig gis thod pa ni//ma mo rnams kyir yang dag bshad//sor mo gnyis bsgreng ral gri ni//srin po bdag nyid chen po'i 'o//
28a1(rgyud): @#//mdzub mo mthe bong sbyar ba ni//chu lha'i zhags pa nyid du bshad//mdzub mo dang ni gung mo dag/rgyal mtshan lta bur legs par brkyang //rlung lha rgyal mtshan phyag rgya ni//lag pa gnyis kyis rab tu brtag/dbu ma gcig pu legs 
28a2(rgyud): brkyang ba//dbyug to'i phyag rgya rab tu bshad//gnod sbyin kun gyi dbang phyug ni//lus ngan bdag nyid chen po'o//mchod phyir thogs kyi phyag rgya ni//tshangs pa yi ru legs par bshad//drag po'i gsal shing shes bya ste//lha mo'i ba Ti sa ru brjod//
28a3(rgyud): tshogs bdag gi ni sna ru bshad//zla ba'i bdud rtsi'i dkyil 'khor lo//nyi ma'i 'od zer stong phrag ni//lag pa gnyis kyis bstan par bya//'od kyi dkyil 'khor sbyor ba yis//dbang po'i rdo rje mtshon cha ltar//'di ni lha rnams kun gyi rgya//dpa' bo 
28a4(rgyud): kun gyis rab tu gnang //dri za rnams kyi phyag rgya ni//glu len lta bur rab tu bsgrags//dga' byed dbang phyug phyag rgya che//rje rnga'i sgra dbyangs rab tu 'bar//sangs rgyas thams cad rab mchod phyir//lha chen pos ni rab tu brtags//ting ting 
28a5(rgyud): cag 'dzin sbyor ba yis//mi'am ci rnams kyis brjod bya//pi wang bsgreng ba'i sbyor ba yis//dbyangs can gyis ni rnam par bshad//rtag tu dun du b+hi sgrog pa//rig pa 'dzin pa rnams kyis brjod//lag gnyis gdengs ka'i tshul byas pa//klu 
28a6(rgyud): rnams kyis ni legs par bshad//gar ni sdud pa'i sbyor ba yis//'gyed dang 'du ba'i de bzhin no//lag pa zhags pa'i nges bcings pas//de ni phyag rgyar rab tu bshad//'di rnams dam tshig phyag rgya ru//de bzhin gshegs 
28a7(rgyud): rnams legs par gsung //las kyi sbyor ba 'bad pa yis//'di rnams kyang ni phyag rgyar bshad//phyag rgya dor nas sems kyi ni//rin chen yang dang yang du bsam//rnam par rtog med gdod nas zhi//chos dbyings mnyam zhing mi mnyam ga/stong pa 
28a8(rgyud): mkha' 'drar shes nas su//mkhas pa yis ni bsgom par bya//'di dag thams cad ma skyes shing //rmi lam lta bur rab tu gnas//dri za'i grong khyer lta bur snang //'tsho ba'i 'jig rten yang dang yang //mig 'phrul lta bur shin du ni//'gro ba thams 
28b1(rgyud): cad rab tu gsal//mi rtag sdug bsngal stong pa ru//srid pa thams cad mdor bsdus pa//bdag med pa zhes mthong nas ni//sangs rgyas kun dang dpa' bo yis//sgyu ma smig rgyu brag cha dang //lbu ba chu yi chu bur dang //gzugs brnyan 
28b2(rgyud): lta bur 'jig rten ni//de ltar sangs rgyas rnams kyis dgongs//thams cad mgal me'i 'khor lo ltar//zo chun khyud mo ji bzhin du//gar gyi gnas gyi tshul 'dra ba//zla ba gnyis dang mtshungs par blta//rtag tu glog gi phreng ba 
28b3(rgyud): 'dra//sbrang bu'i sgra dang mtshungs par gnas//'khor ba shin tu bslang ba ste//rgod ma'i kha dang 'dra bar gnas//'di ltar shes shing rtogs nas ni//bsgom pa'i stobs kyis rang nyid kyis//sems ni legs bsgrub rnal 'byor pa//
28b4(rgyud): de la phyag rgya rab tu bshad//stong pa mtshan ma med pa dang //smon pa med pa'i rang bzhin las//lus dang ngag dang yid kyis ni//dam tshig gsum ni brtags pa'o//phyag rgya sngags dang lha nyid dang //de nyid gsum ni rab tu gnas//bdag nyid 
28b5(rgyud): sbyor ba'i rnal 'byor gyis//mtshan nyid gcig tu rnam par gnas//de 'di dam pa'i phyag rgya ru//sangs rgyas rnams kyis bka' stsal to//phyag rgya yi ge bral ba ste//rnam par med cing snang ba med//dngos po med pa'i chos rnams la//dngos po'i 
28b6(rgyud): de nyid nyid du bshad//dngos med dngos po'i rang bzhin yin//dngos med nyid kyang dngos po yin//dngos dang dngos med rnam par grol//rnal 'byor ye shes mchog tu bde//rtog ge med cing snang ba med/'khrul pa med cing yi ge med//
28b7(rgyud): stong pa nam mkha' mtshungs pa'i sems//gang tshe de ltar nyid gyur pa//de tshe phyag rgya rnams dang sngags//lha nyid du yang rab tu brtag/sngags las phyag rgya ma mthong zhing //phyag rgya las kyang sngags min la//rang nyid lha yi rnal 'byor 
28b8(rgyud): ni//gnyis med sngags dang phyag rgyar brtags//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje snying po rgyan ces bya ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las phyag rgya'i rim par phye ba'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad slar 
29a1(rgyud): @#//yang 'dus par gyur te/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor los sgyur ba chen po rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen po la gsol ba btab pa/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi rigs bskyed par mdzad du gsol/
29a2(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa de dag gis gsol ba btab pa'i tshig nye bar gsan nas 'og min gyi gnas nas byon te dga' ldan gyi gnas su bzhugs nas dga' ldan gyi ris kyi lha'i bu rnams la chos bstan nas zas gtsang gi rigs 
29a3(rgyud): la mngon par gzigs te/mngon par byung bar mdzad nas byang chub kyi snying po dag la nye bar mnan nas nye bar 'khod de//bdud bzhi las rnam par rgyal bar bstan nas byang chub kyi snying po la bzhugs par gyur te//bzhugs nas kyang kun nas 
29a4(rgyud): 'phro ba'i ting nge 'dzin sngon du 'gro ba'i ting nge 'dzin bya ba brgyu stong phrag brgyad cu rtsa bzhi la snyoms par zhugs te/snyoms par zhugs nas kyang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje se gol gyi sgras de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad 
29a5(rgyud): spyan 'dren cing gsang sngags 'di gsungs so////oM sarba ta thA ga ta badzra sa mA dza A kar+Sha ya sarba ta thA ga tA na hUM//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad 'khor dang bcas pa'i dkyil 'khor chen po rnams legs par byon te chos kyi 
29a6(rgyud): dbyings su spros nas phyogs bcu mu med pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad rab 'byam rgya mtsho'i sprin rnams khyab ste/nam mkha'i khams na gnas pa thams cad til gyi gang bu ltar 'dzam bu'i gling du 'khod par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 
29a7(rgyud): 'das rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug gis bkug cing bcug ste/bcings nas dbang du mdzad de//de nyid kyi thugs khar rab tu bzhag par gyur to//de nas bcam ldan 'das rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi gnas dbang phyug chen pos gnyis su med pa'i sbyor bas byin 
29a8(rgyud): gyi brlabs te/sngags 'dis rang gi sems so sor rtogs pa mdzad do//oM tsit+ta pra ti be d+ha~M ka ro mi/de'i rjes thogs nyid la sangs rgyas thams cad kyis//bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs khar longs spyod rdzogs pa dang /
29b1(rgyud): sprul pa'i sku las chos kyi sku rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal ba sems rin po che nges par byung ste//spros par mdzad do//de nyid sems 'od gsal ba//thugs rje chen pos khyab pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi mtshan nyid/
29b2(rgyud): gzung ba dang 'dzin pa'i rnyog pa spangs pa nam mkha' ltar gdod nas zhib//de las thugs rjes chen po la mngon par phyogs par byas nas sems can gyi khams thams cad la rnam par gzigs te/mi shes pas rga ba dang /'chi bas rnam par 
29b3(rgyud): khyab pa'i 'jig rten la gzigs nas slar yang sngags 'dis byang chub kyi sems bskyed pa nye bar bstan pa//oM b+ho d+hi tsit+ta ud pA da yA mi/byang chub kyi sems bskyed pa'i rjes thogs nyid la de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi thugs khar 
29b4(rgyud): sems 'od gsal ba de yongs su rdzogs pa'i zla ba'i dkyil 'khor ltar byin gyis brlabs te/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi thugs khar rnam par gzhag go/legs par rnam par dag pa'i nam mkha' ltar dri ma med pa/spros pa thams 
29b5(rgyud): cad spangs pa me long lta bur brtan par byas nas//gnas te byang chub kyi sems chen po la sngags 'di brjod do//oM dz+nyA na ra smi bi dra dza hU~M/de ni rjes thogs nyid la 'od zer rnam pa sna tshogs pa zla ba'i dkyil 'khor las rnam par 
29b6(rgyud): phyung ngo //de phyung nas kyang chos kyi dbyings kyi pho brang du zhugs te/shes bya'i dkyil 'khor thams cad gsal bar byas nas sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi ri bo'i rtse bsnyil te rab tu rtogs par byas nas gcig tu 'dres te/rdo rje'i sku chen 
29b7(rgyud): po rnams bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs kar rjes su zhugs nas zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi dbus su rab tu gnas so//de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen pos sngags 'dis byin gyis brlabs so//oM tiSh+Tha badzra/slar yang 
29b8(rgyud): sngags 'dis spros pa//oM s+pha ra badzra/slar yang sngags 'dis bsdus so//oM sang ha ra badzra/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis ye shes rdo rje'i snying po 'di ched du brjod pa//oM dz+nyA na badzra/de nas bcom ldan 'das rdorje rnal 
30a1(rgyud): @#//'byor gyi dbang phyug chen pos sngags 'dis byin gyis brlabs pa//oM sarba ta thA ga ta dz+nyA na badzra hU~M/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang /gsung dang /thugs mnyam pa nyid brnyes pas sngags 'dis byin gyis
30a2(rgyud): brlabs pa/oM badzra Ad ma ko haM/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad brtan pa nyid du nye bar gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyis de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi sku dang gsung dang /thugs gzigs nas 
30a3(rgyud): sngags 'dis byin gyis brlab par mdzad do//oM ya thA sarba ta thA ga tasta thA a haM/de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thamad cad kyi sku sems can thams cad kyi don mdzad pa'i rgyu sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sku bdag nyid
30a4(rgyud): sems can gyi khams thams cad du nges par bstan to//de nas brgya byin dang tshangs pa dang //'jig rten skyong ba rnams kyis mthong nas lha dang mi rnams kyis mngon par byang chub pas bcom ldan 'das mngon par byang chub bzhes sgra 
30a5(rgyud): bsgrags so//de nas tshangs pas gsol ba btab ste/chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor nas de dang des 'dul ba'i skye bo btul te gang gis slar ldog pa med pa nyid du gyur to//'di rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi byang chub bsgom pa'i rim pa'o//
30a6(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs nas sangs rgyas kyi byang chub pa bstan te sems can thams cad kyi ngon mdzad pa nye bar bsgrubs nas de'i thugs ka las rdo rje phyung ste nyid kyi phyag tu gzhag par 
30a7(rgyud): gyur te//de nas 'od zer rnam par phyung ste/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad snang bar mdzad nas slar log ste//bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs khar zhugs nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po'i skur byin gyis brlabs
30a8(rgyud): te mtshan nyid thams cad yongs su rdzogs par rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa/dar dang phreng bas dbang bskur ba//zla ba'i kha tog lta bu'i 'od zer can du byin gyis brlabs te rnam par gnas so//de nas thugs kha nas phyung nas
30b1(rgyud): zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la bsten te/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyan snga dang //rgyab dang drung du rdo rje sems dpa' dang //rdo rje chos dang rdo rje rin chen dang //rdo rje las la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' chen po'i phreng bar rnam par 
30b2(rgyud): gnas so//de bzhin du sgeg mo la sogs pa rnams dang bdug pa la sogs pa'i lha khyad par du nges par bskyed cing gzugs la sogs pa rnams dang lcags kyu la sogs pa'i lha rnams bskyed de 'di dag gi dkyil 'khor bka' 
30b3(rgyud): stsal pa/de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//dkyil 'khor chen po mchog gyur pa//sangs rgyas dpa' bo thams cad kyis//rdo rje snying po zhes su gsungs//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//dar dang me tog phreng bas brgyan//sgo khyud dang ni kha khyer dang //
30b4(rgyud): rta babs kyis ni so sor spras//de yi dbus su khri chen po//kun nas yongs su gru bzhi pa//de yi steng du pad chen po//ge sar ldan zhing rab mdzes pa//de yi lte ba'i dbus su ni//sangs rgyas sku gzugs dgod par bya//sangs rgyas kyi ni drung 
30b5(rgyud): kun du//sangs rgyas thams cad dgod par bya//sangs rgya kun gyi nye 'khor du//'khor los sgyur ba la sogs bri//phyi yi gnas ni thams cad du//sangs rgyas sras po rnam par dgod//kha dog ser po gzi brjid che//dbang chen dkyil 
30b6(rgyud): 'khor rab 'bar ba//thams cad rnam pa sna tshogs rgyan//brgyan pa de dag dgod par bya//rdo rje snying po rgyan gyi rgyud chen po las dbang chen gyi dkyil 'khor gyi cho ga'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyis slar yang 
30b7(rgyud): chu'i lha'i dkyil 'khor chen po bka' stsal pa//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//dkyil 'khor chen po mchog gyur pa//chos kyi 'khor lo 'dra bas na//chos kyi 'khor lo zhes su brjod//de yi phyi rol gnyis pa la//zlum po ru ni rtogs 
30b8(rgyud): par bya//ri mo mang po sna tshogs pas//sgo dang sgo khyud brtag par bya//rta babs rnam pa sna tshogs pa//dar dang me tog phreng bas brgyan//de yi dbus su pad chen po//ge sar ldan zhing rab mdzes pa//'dab ma brgyad kyi ka ba'i mdun//
31a1(rgyud): dkyil 'khor brgyad kyis brgyan pa'o//dkyil 'khor de yi dbus su ni//rgyal ba thams cad dkar por mchog/kha ba bzho ltar dkar gsal//shel dang zla ba lta bu'i 'od//kun da zla ba ltar dkar brtan//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus 
31a2(rgyud): son par//de yi nye 'khor thams cad du//sangs rgyas sras po rnam par dgod//thams cad zla ba ltar dkar zhing //zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas pa//mtshon cha sna tshogs zla ba 'dra//zla ba 'bar mtshungs mnyam med pa//chu yi ming zhes bya ba ya//
31a3(rgyud): dkyil 'khor dam pa nyid du 'dod//rdo rje snying po rgyan gyi rgyud chen po las chu lha'i dkyil 'khor gyi cho ga'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen pos me lha zhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po bka' stsal pa/de nas yang 
31a4(rgyud): dag bshad bya ba//dkyil 'khor chen po mchog gyur pa//rdo rje ye shes gzi brjid mtshungs//rdo rje gzi brjid ces su brjod//gru gsum rnam pa nyid du bya//sum mdo ris kyi rnam pa mtshungs//'bar ba'i phreng 'khrugs 'jigs pa'o//kha dog 
31a5(rgyud): dmar po stobs po che//'jig pa'i dus kyi me 'dra ba//rtag tu pad ma rA ga mtshungs//de yi dbus su gzi brjid che//nyi ma'i kha dog stobs po che//rdo rje rtse gsum sna tshogs pa//sum mdo'i snying khar gzhag par bya//bal po se'u'i me tog 
31a6(rgyud): mtshungs//ya ba'i me tog dag dang 'dra//pad ma pad ma rA ga mtshungs/sangs rgyas dpa' bo yang dag 'bar//bzang po mchal dang mtshungs 'dra ba//rgya skyags bcud dang 'dra bar gnas//de yi nye 'khor thams cad du//de bzhin sangs 
31a7(rgyud): rgyas sangs rgyas sras//khro bo dang ni ma dag pa dag/mkhas pa yis ni dgod par bya//de dag thams cad kha dog dmar//gzi brjid khams la 'od ldan pa//'od zer dmar po rab 'phro ba//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus son par//
31a8(rgyud): thams cad dmar po'i mtshon cha can//dmar po'i rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan//dmar po'i gos kyis bkab pa'i lus//phreng ba dmar po mchog 'dzin pa//'di ni gzi brjid gter zhes bya//dkyil 'khor mchog tu 'bar ba'o//rdo rje 
31b1(rgyud): snying po rgyan gyi rgyud chen po las me lha zhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po rdzogs s+ho////
32a1(rgyud): @#//phyug chen po de rnams kyis gsol ba btab pa'i tshig nye bar gsan nas sgrub pa'i thabs kyis cho ga mchog tu gsang ba chen po bka' stsal pa/sgeg mo sogs pas mchod pa kun//yang dag mdor bsdus byas nas 
32a2(rgyud): ni//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku chen por//bdag gis rtag tu bsgom par bya//sngags kyi yi ge yang dag ldan//'dod pa ster ba'i sku ru bya//sgra dang gzugs dang de bzhin sbyor//yi ger phreng ba de bzhin te//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus 
32a3(rgyud): gnas par//sngags pas thams cad rnam par bsam//hU~M/sngags pas snying khar son pa bsgom//gsang sngags dge ba brjod nas su//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs dag pa//dung gi sgra ru rab tu brjod//dril bu'i sgra ni rab 
32a4(rgyud): gsal ba//pad mo rnal 'byor dbang phyug go//dus min sprin gyi hU~M gi sgra//tsa Ta tsa Ta'i sgra 'phro ba//rdo rje rin chen dbang phyug gis//sgra ru de bzhin gshegs rnams gsungs//nor bu las kyi rigs kyi yang //rnga chen sgra ru rab tu bshad//
32a5(rgyud): pi wang gling pu de bzhin glu//gar gyi rgyan gyi sgra dang ni//rdza rnga'i ngar sgra hU~M gi sgra//spyi yi nyid du rab tu bshad//rtog pa kun la rnam par sbyar//sgrub pa'i thabs kyi las rnams dang //de nas gzugs kyi bzlas pa mchog/go rims ji 
32a6(rgyud): bzhin rab tu bshad//dgug dang gzhug dang bcing ba dang //lha rnams dbang du bya ba ste//de la rang gi tshul rab sbyar//sngags dang phyag rgya rab tu sbyar//de rjes rnam pa kun gyi mchog/rtag tu lus chen bsam bya bsam//lha yi sku 
32a7(rgyud): gzugs bsgom par bya//lag pa'i phyag rgya rab nges pa'i//phyag rgya'ang snying khar son zhes bya//rang gi rigs rim cho ga yis//de yi lus ni 'od gsal zhing //sgron ma'i 'od ltar gsal ba'i khyim//de ltar kun nas thams cad du//
32a8(rgyud): kun nas ba spu'i rtse dag nas//'od zer 'bar ba'i phreng dag pas//gzugs kyi bzlas pa zhes su bshad//de nas sbyor ba'i bzlas pa ni//gsang sngags sgrub pos brtsam par bya//mchod dang byin rlabs dbang bskur mchog/spro la stsogs 
32a9(rgyud): ris thams cad du//sbyor ba'i bzlas pa gtso bo 'di//'bad pa yid ni rab tu bshad//snying kha mgrin pa mchu lce ma ni//rkan dang spyi bo nyid ma yin//nad min phyi dang gnyis par min//chos ni 'ba' zhig kho na la//bzlas pa'i sgra yang 
32b1(rgyud): 'byung ma yin//'di ni bcom ldan rnal 'byor che//shes rab pha rol phyin gsal ba//bsgom pa'i bsngags pa de nyid ni//rnal 'byor pa yi gnas su bshad//de nas steng du mdor bsdus te//snying kha'i dkyil 'khor rab zlum pa//zla ba gang ba 
32b2(rgyud): ltar bsam bya//dri ma med cing legs par gnas//de la yi ge rnam par dgod//tshig gi phreng ba ma chad pa//mu tig phreng ba ltar rgyus pa//char sprin gyi ni me 'dra'i 'od//de tshe thams cad gsal bar byed//lha yi lus ni snang byed cing //ji lta 
32b3(rgyud): ji ltar yi ge'i phreng //mchog gi lus la rab 'jug pa//de lta de ltar sngags kyi lus//lha yi bdag nyid nyid du 'gyur//mthu stobs lha dbang thams cad ni//tshangs pa khyab 'jug las cher lhag/bzlas pa de nyid mchog 'di ni//sangs rgyas 
32b4(rgyud): kun gyis bsngags pa'o//ji ltar sems ni bsgom pa yis//de lta dngos grub mchog tu 'gyur//rdo rje snying po rgyan gyi rgyud chen po las bzlas pa'i de kho na nyid kyi le'u'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen pos 
32b5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bstod pa rin po che bka' stsal pa/mnyam med mig yo mnyam med mchog gi chos nyid can//thugs rje'i bdag nyid 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal sel bar mdzad//yon tan mtha' yas dngos grub thams cad sbyin 
32b6(rgyud): mdzad pa//dri med mi g.yo mnyam med dam pa'i mchog chos can//yon tan shin tu phra ba'i tshal 'ang tshad med de//dpe ni nam mkha' dang yang bskrun du yod ma yin//dngos grub mtha' yas mnyam med dpe dang bral ba rnams//sems can khams 
32b7(rgyud): la dngos grub mchog rnams nges par stsol//rtag tu dri ma med pa thugs rje'i shugs las byung //smon lam grub pa 'gog pa med pa'i chos nyid can//'gro ba'i don grub rab tu brtson pa mtha' yas pa//thugs rje chen po'i bdag nyid rtag tu 
32b8(rgyud): rnam par snang //tshad med tshad dang bral ba'i shin tu rdzogs pa yi//bde bar gshegs pa go 'phang dam pa brnyes gyur kyang //khams gsum rnams la dngos grub mchog stsol mdzad pa yi//thugs rje'i spyod pa mi g.yo 'gag par yod 
32b9(rgyud): mi 'gyur//e ma'o shin tu bzang po'i chos nyid mchog dang ldan//mchog sbyin rnams kyi nang na go 'phang mchog brnyes pa//khams gsum kun la rtag tu dngos grub mchog stsol mdzod//mgon po dus gsum sgrub pa med pa rab 
33a1(rgyud): @#//mkhyen pas//dam tshig mchog gsum dngos grub dam pa bdag la gsol//'di de sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//yang dag yon tan rgyas pa ste//lan cig tsam zhig brjod pas kyang //gsang sngags thams cad 'grub 
33a2(rgyud): par 'gyur//bstod pa'i rgyal po 'di yis ni//de bzhin gshegs de kun bstod na//rab yangs dngos grub stsol 'gyur te//bskal par gnas shing bskal par bskul//lus ni g.yo ba med pa ru bdag nyid brtan par byas pa yis//mgon 
33a3(rgyud): po chen po rnam snang mdzad//mi skyod rin chen 'byung gnas dang //rgyal ba dag pa 'od dpag med//don grub rgyal po thams cad du//dngul chu bcud len de nyid dang //mchog kyang rab tu gsol bar byed//dngos grub yid 'ong ma lus pa//rgyas 
33a4(rgyud): pa'i don dang phun sum tshogs//re ba thams cad yongs rdzogs pa//yid la 'dod pa ster bar byed//ye shes tshe dang stobs dang shugs//mchog tu dge ba gsol bar mdzad//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bstod pa'i rgyal po zhes 
33a5(rgyud): bya ba rdzogs s+ho////de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen pos de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi dngos grub kyi cho ga rgyas pa bka' stsal pa/chos grangs med cing rgyu skar med//smyung bar gnas pa'i cho ga med//ji 
33a6(rgyud): ltar bshad pa tshogs bslab pa'i gnas nye bar gnas bzung ste/gang yang rung ba'i yid dga' bar ji ltar rung ba'i dkyil 'khor byas ste/me tog sil ma bkram la bdug spos kyis bdugs te/de bzhin gshegs pa thams bcad la ji ltar nus pa'i 
33a7(rgyud): me tog la sogs pa'i mchod pa rnams kyi mchod par byas te/sngags 'dis yid kyis kyang sdig pa bshags te/oM sarba pA pA ta kA yi ka bA tsi ka ma na sod b+hA tAn rA ga/dwe Sha mo ha ma d+ha mA na d+hi Sh+The sha yang kr-i tra~M tan tre kA li ka~M hU~M hU~M hU~M 
33a8(rgyud): phaT phaT phaT sar+b+ba bA bA~M s+pho Ta yA mi swA hA/oM sar+b+ba bA bA pra ti de sha yA mi swA hA/de'i rjes thogs la bdag nyid kyi dngos po dbul bar bya ste/oM sar+b+ba ta thA ga ta nI na A ta ma b+hA ba ni ra yA ta yA mi/sar+b+ba ta thA ga tAHpra ti gr-i h+na na tu mi swA hA 
33b1(rgyud): de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bsod nams la rjes su yi rang ba ni//oM sar+b+ba ta thA ga ta bu N+ya dz+nyA na saM b+hA/rA Na a nu mo da yA mi swA hA/yang dang yang du byang chub kyi sems bskyed pa rang bzhin gyis grub pa'i sngags kyis 
33b2(rgyud): bya ste/oM sar+b+ba ta thA ga tas man ta b+ha dra ma hA badzra sa ma ya~M ma hA bo d+hi tsit+ta ud bA da yA mi swA hA/ma nas dan tsin ta pe ta/de nas byang chub kyi sems bskyed ba'i rjes thogs la zla ba'i kha dog can zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la bzhugs pa'i bsod nams dang ye shes 
33b3(rgyud): kyi tshogs yongs su rdzogs pas bcu'i dbang phyug nor bu rin po che chen po'i pad ma'i snying po seng ge'i khri'i steng du bdag nyid bsam par bya'o//oM bud d+ho haM/sngags 'dis bdag yongs su dag par byas te/oM swa b+hA ba shud+d+hAH
33b4(rgyud): sar+b+ba ta thA ga taHswA b+hA ba shud d+ho haM/sngags 'dis ba dag nyid chos kyi dbyings yangs pa 'od gsal ba'i snang bar shin du gnas par bya ste/oM d+harmA d+hA tu swa b+hA ba Ata ma ko haM/de nas skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis kyis brgyan 
33b5(rgyud): par bdag nyid byin gyis brlabs te 'jig rten thams cad las shin tu 'das pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi skur bdag nyid sngags 'dis gnas par bya ste/oM bra b+hi thA do ha~M/de bzhin du rim pa 'dis chos kyi dbyings 
33b6(rgyud): rgya chen por spros nas bdag nyid sangs rgyas nyid do//zhes nga rgyal bskyed la bdag skye dgu ma lus pa'i dpung gnyen du byas la/chos kyi dbyings yangs pa'od gsal bar lhag par gnas par bya ste nye bar rgyas par thun mtshams 
33b7(rgyud): bzhir bzlas pa brtsam par bya ste/thun mtshams gsum du dus ji lta bar yi ge la rjes su gzhol ba'i yid kyis 'bum phrag nyi shu bzlas par bya zhing nyi ma re rar stong phrag brgyad dam stong phrag re bzlas te ji srid mtshan ma thob 
33b8(rgyud): pa de srid du'o//tsan dan dang ga bur dang /gur gum la sogs pa rnams kyis dkyil 'khor byas te/nye bar mkho ba thams cad kyis mchod pa byas nas cho 'phrul gyi zla ba'am sa ga'i nye gdab pa mar gyi mar me stong phrag brgyad spar la rdo rje

40b4(rgyud): nyid//rnal 'byor ye shes phun sum tshogs//skye ba 'di la 'grub 'gyur te//'di la the tshom mi bya'o//dbyangs dang yi ge thams cad la//sbyor ba'i dbye bas rnam par dbye//tshig dang ngag gi tshad rnams dang //ting nge 'dzin dang sngags

53a7(rgyud): spyan ni 'grub par 'gyur//mig gi phyag rgya rab tu sbyar//mig gi phyag rgya 'di shin du//'jig rten gsum yang gar byed do//sangs rgyas spyan gyi phyag rgya'i sgrub pa'i thabs so//chu yi dkyil 'khor dgyes pa na//bcud kyis len 
53a8(rgyud): dang mtshungs pa'i mchog/mtha' yas rgyas shing yangs pa yi//dri ma med pa'i mkha' la gnas//dkyil 'khor de yi dbus su ni//lha mo mchog tu mdzes pa mo//'dam bu mda' rgyu'i mdog ltar dkar//grub ma ral pa'i cod 
53b1(rgyud): ban 'chad//'gro ba mtha' dag snang mdzad ma//rtag tu nam mkha' bzhin du gnas//sngags ni/oM ka Te bi ka Te ka taM ka Te swA hA/gos dkar mo yi phyag rgya che//dung dang kun da zla ba 'dra//sngags kyi yi ge'i phreng ba che//pad ma'i rgyal po rab 
53b2(rgyud): tu gnas//'gro ba mtha' dag snangs mdzad na//sgrub pa pos ni sgrub par bya//dam pa'i lha mo 'grub par 'gyur//pad ma'i dkyil 'khor sgrub pa'i thabs//zhi ba'i ro dang yang dag ldan//zhi ba'i yi ge mnyam par ldan//zhi bar mdzad cing 'jig 
53b3(rgyud): rten gyi//sa gsum sgrub par byed pa'i thabs//gos dkar mo'i sgrub pa'i thabs so//sngo bsangs dkar mo sna tshogs gzugs//rnam pa sna tshogs rgyan gyis brgyan//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas pa//sgeg pa'i nyams kyis 
53b4(rgyud): yongs gang ba//'gro ba mtha' dag skyob mdzad ma//thugs rje'i thugs dang ldan pa can//rab tu 'bad de bsgrub par bya//rol pa yi ni yi ges mchod//sngags ni/oM tA re tut+tA re tu re swA hA/chos kyi phyag rgya mtha' yas mchog/
53b5(rgyud): sgrol mas legs par rab tu gnas//gsang chen bzhin du thams cad la//bcud kyis len dang 'dra ba'i mchog/kun du kun tshe thams cad du//dngos grub thams cad stsol bar mdzad//sgrol ma'i sgrub pa'i thabs so//dkyil 
53b6(rgyud): 'khor chen po'i dbus su ni//rnal 'byor dbang phyug chen mo mchog/phyag bzhi spyan ni gsum pa dang //ral pa'i cod pan dag gis brgyan//'dam bu mda' rgyu'i mdog ltar dkar//dpa' bo la sogs nyams kyis brgyan//brtson

54a1(rgyud): @#//gsang sngags kun gyi bdag nyid mchog/'gro ba kun kyang 'gugs byed na//gdug pa'i sems can lta ci dgos//khro gnyer can gyi sgrub pa'i thabs so////nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor

54a6(rgyud): oM ku lan d+ha ri/b+han d+ha b+han d+ha hU~M phaT/gsang sngags 'di ni don gyi mchog/sangs rgyas kun gyis gsungs pa yin//sa gsum po ni 'gugs byed cing //tshangs pa la sogs lha rnams kun//'gro ba thams cad 'gugs byed la//rang 
54a7(rgyud): gi dbang du gnas pa'ang yin//mA ma kI sgrub pa'i thabs so////rlung gi dkyil 'khor drag po la//rdo rje lu gu rgyud kyis bskor//de yi dbus su snang ba che//zla ba'i gzugs kyis rnam par brgyan//des spros pa las bdag nyid ni//rdo rje lu gu 
54a8(rgyud): rgyud kyi sku//phyag bzhi ma la lha mo de//rdo rje yi ni rgyan gyis brgyan//dmar po 'bar ba de yis ni//sa gsum po ni 'ching bar mdzad//tshangs pa khyab 'jug dbang phyug che//bkug nas dbang du mdzad par gnas//dngos grub thams cad 'dzin 
54b1(rgyud): pa dang /de yi thugs khar rdo rje che//'bar ba'i phreng ba 'khrigs pa'i 'od//de yi 'bar ba lcags sgrog gi/rnam pa rdo rje'i mtshan mas mtshan//nges par phyung nas de dag kun//chos dbyings mnyam dang ma mnyam pa'i//'jig rten khams ni 
54b2(rgyud): mtha' dag tu//kun nas rnam par bkang nas kyang //bsgom bya rdo rje thams cad du//khro bo dang ldan spro ba'i zhal//lto ba 'phyang zhing sku che ba//mche ba bzhi yis rnam par brgyan//'bar ba yi ni phreng ba gsal//gzugs mdzes drag po'i 
54b3(rgyud): gzugs dang ldan//sngags ni/oM badzra shrI kha lA yA dI be ta ye/ma hA ba lA ya/ma hA bi r+yA rA dzA ye tre pra dz+wa li ta/hastA ya na mo nI la kaN+Da ya ki rA ta rU pa la m+bo darA ya/b+hi ku TI mu khA ya/mA dza ra mu khA ya/tsa tu daMSh+TAM ka ra lA ya/e h+ye hi 
54b4(rgyud): a s+ya sha rI ra/ma nu pra bi sh+ya shi ra gr-I h+na grI ba grIh+na/hasta bA daM gr-i h+na ba Sha/A be sha ya/kaM ba ya/kaM ba ya/b+ha ma ya/maN+Da la ma d+he/bi ku r+tha bi ku r+tha/tsA r+N+Na tsA r+N+Na ku Ta ku TA s+pho Ta ya s+pho Ta ya/ma rmA Ni naM bi n+ta ya/hri da ya/ts+tshin+ta ba la ba la/kiny+tsi 
54b5(rgyud): rA ya si/de ga nA ga/yak+Sha rA k+Sha sa/pi shA tsa na trA ya/sa ma ya ma nu sma ra/dzA ti ma nu sma ra hA hA ni d+hi ni d+hi/ni Ni ni Ni/badzra shrIM kha lA Adz+nyA pa yi taM swA hA/oM badzra bA Ni hU~M phaT/oM badzri ni hU~M phaT/oM badzra kA li hU~M phaT swA hA//

56a1(rgyud): @#//

57a1(rgyud): @#//nyid du dmigs pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la mnyam par bzhag nas mnyam par bzhag pa'i de ma thag tu yang bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs rdo rje la rjes su btsug nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang

57a3(rgyud): 'dzin las bzhengs te/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi theg pa chen po mngon par rtogs pa zhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po yod do zhes gsungs

57b2(rgyud): stsal pa/da nas dkyil 'khor chen po mchog/yang dag rab tu bshad bya ba//dam tshig chen po rdo rje mchog/snying po chen po'i rgyan ces bya//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//rta babs bzhis ni mdzes par byas//thig bzhi dang ni mnyam par

58b3(rgyud): sangs rgyas ye shes bla na me//gang zhig brtan phyir lus min yang //sangs rgyas sku nyid du gyur to//de nas rdo rje'i sku byang chub sems dpa' chen pa bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs kha nas 'thon nas bcom ldan 'das mi bskyod pa'i 
58b4(rgyud): nye 'khor thams cad rnam pa bzhis phyi ste//dkar po ser po/dmar po ljang khu'i mdog tu mngon par sprul nas rang gi sngags kyi yi ges zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la brten te gnas par gyur to//dgug pa'i ting nge 'dzin ni//oM badzra rA
